{"parentUuid":"888628d3-f12d-483b-8411-b0d36fef2ce2","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"e41df3f2-cb2a-4f0e-afbe-0932c0b0d501","timestamp":"2026-05-09T12:30:04.482Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"e41df3f2-cb2a-4f0e-afbe-0932c0b0d501","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. {"parentUuid":"bec8c6f1-7cfa-429d-a5bf-b9b671739018","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"ac939f07-50ad-4ebc-8be9-4b35303893fc","timestamp":"2026-05-09T12:30:12.317Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"f55e9eec-09f2-4736-95e9-46d2f4039723","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"079aebaa-bf67-4e85-8846-668508ad386d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UHkj4u7dsvxTsKei9igzyR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Sync-Bundle Template — Specialized Q&A for \"Are We Still Aligned?\"\n2\t\n3\t> Specialized Q&A template for periodic sync-grilling rounds. Differs from feature/scope grill:\n4\t> questions verify shared understanding rather than propose new direction.\n5\t> Created S7 (2026-04-29) per Track 5.5b.4 (D-003 § 5.5b.4).\n6\t> Pairs with `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` SessionStart hook (Track 5.5b.3).\n7\t\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t## When to use this template\n11\t\n12\tUse when ANY of:\n13\t- `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` emits SYNC-GRILLING DUE in additionalContext\n14\t- ≥3 items in `sync-state.md` have `state: assumed-aligned` for >7 days without re-confirmation\n15\t- ≥1 item in `sync-state.md` has `state: open-question` blocking active track\n16\t- Phase boundary about to fire (post-Phase-N close, pre-Phase-(N+1) entry)\n17\t\n18\tDo NOT use when:\n19\t- All sync-state items are `confirmed-aligned` and recent (<7 days)\n20\t- Active track requires immediate scope-tier or charter-tier decisions (use feature/scope grill instead)\n21\t\n22\t---\n23\t\n24\t## Composition rules (BINDING per UP-06 NO-Silent-Default)\n25\t\n26\t1. **Source items only from `agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md`**. Do NOT invent new sync questions ad-hoc — those belong in feature/scope grill.\n27\t\n28\t2. **Phrase every question as alignment check**, not new direction. Patterns:\n29\t   - ✅ \"Do we still understand X the same way?\"\n30\t   - ✅ \"Has Y changed since we last confirmed it on {date}?\"\n31\t   - ✅ \"Is Z still the operational policy, or has practice diverged?\"\n32\t   - ❌ \"Should we change X?\" (this is feature/scope, not sync)\n33\t   - ❌ \"What direction for Y?\" (open-question, not alignment check)\n34\t\n35\t3. **Each question MUST cite exactly ONE sync-state item ID** (sync-NNN). Multi-item questions get split.\n36\t\n37\t4. **Multi-batch protocol**: max 4 questions per `AskUserQuestion` call. Bundle of N>4 sync items → ceil(N/4) batches across same session or sequential turns. NEVER use file-only-with-default-acceptance fallback (UP-06 hard rule).\n38\t\n39\t5. **Each option = single explicit pick**:\n40\t   - Option A: \"Yes, still aligned — confirmation re-stamp\"\n41\t   - Option B: \"No, my understanding has shifted — let me explain (other answer)\"\n42\t   - Option C (only when applicable): \"Partially — still aligned on X but Y has changed\"\n43\t   - NEVER use vague labels like \"I'm not sure\" or \"default applies after 24h\"\n44\t\n45\t6. **Closure protocol**: after answers received:\n46\t   - If A → update sync-state.md item `state: confirmed-aligned`, `confirmed_at: <today>`, `confirmation_via: AskUserQuestion sync-grill <date>`\n47\t   - If B → transition `state: open-question`, append `gap_note: <user explanation>`, queue follow-up via `queued-grill-master.md` with explicit `fire_when:` trigger\n48\t   - If C → split into two items; one confirmed, one open-question\n49\t\n50\t---\n51\t\n52\t## Bundle skeleton (use as-is, fill blanks)\n53\t\n54\t```markdown\n55\t## Sync Bundle — <YYYY-MM-DD> (S<N>)\n56\t\n57\t**Trigger**: <sync-grilling-trigger.sh fired | manual | phase-boundary>\n58\t**Last sync check**: <date from sync-state.md>\n59\t**Items checked this round** (N=<count>): sync-<id1>, sync-<id2>, ...\n60\t\n61\t### Q1 — sync-<NNN>: <statement from sync-state.md>\n62\t**Original confirmation date**: <date>\n63\t**Original basis**: <decision/AskUserQuestion ref>\n64\t\n65\tQuestion: \"Do we still understand <thing> the same way as <date>?\"\n66\tOptions:\n67\t- A: Yes, still aligned (confirmation re-stamp)\n68\t- B: No, my understanding has shifted — let me explain\n69\t- C (if applicable): Partially aligned — see notes\n70\t\n71\t### Q2 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n72\t[same format as Q1]\n73\t\n74\t### Q3 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n75\t[same format]\n76\t\n77\t### Q4 — sync-<NNN>: ...\n78\t[same format]\n79\t```\n80\t\n81\t---\n82\t\n83\t## Anti-patterns to avoid\n84\t\n85\t| Anti-pattern | Why bad | Correct |\n86\t|---|---|---|\n87\t| Mix sync + scope questions in same bundle | UP-06 charter-tier split rule (mixing dilutes alignment signal) | Separate bundles |\n88\t| Question without `sync-NNN` reference | sync-state.md becomes drift-prone | Always cite item ID |\n89\t| Ask 8+ sync questions in one batch | AskUserQuestion 4-limit + cognitive overload | Multi-batch |\n90\t| Use file-only \"answer by editing this file\" fallback | UP-06 silent-default rule | AskUserQuestion is PRIMARY |\n91\t| Omit `confirmation_via:` field on update | Lose audit trail | Always cite the AskUserQuestion turn |\n92\t\n93\t---\n94\t\n95\t## Worked example (4-question round, derived from sync-state.md current state)\n96\t\n97\t```\n98\t## Sync Bundle — 2026-05-10 (S10, post-Track 6 entry)\n99\t\n100\t**Trigger**: sync-grilling-trigger.sh fired (4 sessions since last check; Differs from feature/scope grill:\n> questions verify shared understanding rather than propose new direction.\n> Created S7 (2026-04-29) per Track 5.5b.4 (D-003 § 5.5b.4).\n> Pairs with `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` SessionStart hook (Track 5.5b.3).\n\n---\n\n## When to use this template\n\nUse when ANY of:\n- `sync-grilling-trigger.sh` emits SYNC-GRILLING DUE in additionalContext\n- ≥3 items in `sync-state.md` have `state: assumed-aligned` for >7 days without re-confirmation\n- ≥1 item in `sync-state.md` has `state: open-question` blocking active track\n- Phase boundary about to fire (post-Phase-N close, pre-Phase-(N+1) entry)\n\nDo NOT use when:\n- All sync-state items are `confirmed-aligned` and recent (<7 days)\n- Active track requires immediate scope-tier or charter-tier decisions (use feature/scope grill instead)\n\n---\n\n## Composition rules (BINDING per UP-06 NO-Silent-Default)\n\n1. **Source items only from `agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md`**. Do NOT invent new sync questions ad-hoc — those belong in feature/scope grill.\n\n2. **Phrase every question as alignment check**, not new direction. Patterns:\n   - ✅ \"Do we still understand X the same way?\"\n   - ✅ \"Has Y changed since we last confirmed it on {date}?\"\n   - ✅ \"Is Z still the operational policy, or has practice diverged?\"\n   - ❌ \"Should we change X?\" (this is feature/scope, not sync)\n   - ❌ \"What direction for Y?\" (open-question, not alignment check)\n\n3. **Each question MUST cite exactly ONE sync-state item ID** (sync-NNN). Multi-item questions get split.\n\n4. **Multi-batch protocol**: max 4 questions per `AskUserQuestion` call. Bundle of N>4 sync items → ceil(N/4) batches across same session or sequential turns. NEVER use file-only-with-default-acceptance fallback (UP-06 hard rule).\n\n5. **Each option = single explicit pick**:\n   - Option A: \"Yes, still aligned — confirmation re-stamp\"\n   - Option B: \"No, my understanding has shifted — let me explain (other answer)\"\n   - Option C (only when applicable): \"Partially — still aligned on X but Y has changed\"\n   - NEVER use vague labels like \"I'm not sure\" or \"default applies after 24h\"\n\n6. **Closure protocol**: after answers received:\n   - If A → update sync-state.md item `state: confirmed-aligned`, `confirmed_at: <today>`, `confirmation_via: AskUserQuestion sync-grill <date>`\n   - If B → transition `state: open-question`, append `gap_note: <user explanation>`, queue follow-up via `queued-grill-master.md` with explicit `fire_when:` trigger\n   - If C → split into two items; {"parentUuid":"f08e9c36-9a28-42fd-8019-feade7de50b7","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01L2tGxF3BpkQtaqnmcsNE85","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018b9PAqiUDgUdvZ5iHEvGiz","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\memory\\anthropic_api_to_subagent.md","content":"---\nname: Replace ANTHROPIC_API_KEY with Claude Code subagent\ndescription: For every place that uses ANTHROPIC_API_KEY / anthropic SDK direct calls, refactor to Claude Code subagent dispatch (Claude subscription billing, not API metered)\ntype: feedback\n---\n\nRule: For EVERY `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` env var reference and every direct `anthropic` SDK call in stockforge, refactor to use a Claude Code subagent dispatch (Agent tool with subagent_type) — i.e., billed via Claude Pro/Max/Team subscription, NOT metered per-token via Anthropic API. This applies systemically (production runtime + dogfood + tests + tooling), not just one-off.\n\n**Why:** User has Claude Code subscription (Pro/Max). Direct Anthropic API calls would be redundant per-token billing on top of an already-paid subscription. Stated verbatim by user 2026-05-09 during S227 LIVE-dogfood gate decision: \"no need anthropic api key, use claude code subagent (claude subscription) instead, for every 'anthropic api key'\". Phrase \"for every\" makes this a generalized systemic rule, not one-off override.\n\n**How to apply:**\n- Production code: when designing LLM calls (claim extraction, thesis critique, KOL summarization, sentiment analysis, etc.), do NOT plan around `anthropic` SDK / `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. Instead, design a subagent-dispatch pattern (Agent tool from Claude Code; possibly via `claude --print` CLI shell-out for non-interactive contexts, OR define a custom subagent type).\n- Dogfood acceptance: cost model is now ~$0 marginal (subscription already paid) instead of $8-15. Re-fire LIVE-dogfood gate once subagent pattern is wired.\n- Existing code: enumerate all `anthropic` imports + `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` references → refactor as subagent dispatch.\n- New code: NEVER add `anthropic` SDK import or `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` reference. If you see one in a draft, flag and replace.\n- Mock LLM (`STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=true`) remains valid for unit tests.\n- Charter implication: likely needs ADR or charter amendment codifying this routing decision.\n\n**Edge cases:**\n- If a runtime context cannot reach Claude Code (e.g., headless Docker container, CI), revisit with user before falling back to API.\n- Tests that verify \"API call made\" should mock the subagent dispatch path, not anthropic SDK.\n\n**Date asserted:** 2026-05-09 (S227)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":104865,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":2153,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":104865,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":2153,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":104865,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":104865},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CasCGNDMiaQ7qJ8488jNq","type":"assistant","uuid":"686f65d0-c02e-450d-abf7-71b94d1b2994","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:00:27.341Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"9f1e82af-0b27-4dc9-ab76-6823c9d97f69","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"079aebaa-bf67-4e85-8846-668508ad386d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Y38oVCLA1UFUVMJPZug2zM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Subagent transport — claude CLI subprocess as zero-marginal-cost LLM substrate.\n2\t\n3\tDrop-in replacement for the default `transport` callable in\n4\t`ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter`. Bills against Claude Code subscription\n5\t(parent OAuth/keychain) — no separate ANTHROPIC_API_KEY required.\n6\t\n7\tSource: agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md § \"User insight mid-S38\"\n8\t        + L-S38-2 lesson candidate (subagent dispatch as LLM substrate)\n9\t        + S43b probe 2026-05-01 (research/r-2026-05-01-claude-cli-substrate.md)\n10\t\n11\tMechanism:\n12\t    subprocess.run([\n13\t        \"claude\", \"-p\",\n14\t        \"--model\", <model>,\n15\t        \"--output-format\", \"json\",\n16\t        \"--disable-slash-commands\",\n17\t        \"--system-prompt\", <system_prompt>,\n18\t    ], input=<user_message>, ...)\n19\t\n20\tReturns CLI JSON envelope:\n21\t    {\"result\": \"<model output (often wrapped in ```json fence)>\",\n22\t     \"total_cost_usd\": <float>,\n23\t     \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\", \"output_tokens\",\n24\t               \"cache_creation_input_tokens\", \"cache_read_input_tokens\"},\n25\t     \"is_error\": <bool>, ...}\n26\t\n27\tAdapter signature returns (text, input_tokens, output_tokens). We aggregate\n28\tinput_tokens = input + cache_creation + cache_read (cache_read charged at\n29\t~10% but bundled approximation is sufficient for cost tracking; exact\n30\ttotal_cost_usd is logged separately for audit).\n31\t\n32\tTrade-offs vs anthropic SDK direct:\n33\t- (+) No API key required; bills against subscription\n34\t- (+) Exact cost reported by CLI (logged at log.info)\n35\t- (-) No `temperature` control — CLI uses model defaults\n36\t- (-) Per-call latency 15-30s (subprocess overhead + CLAUDE.md cache creation)\n37\t- (-) Response often wrapped in ```json fence — stripped by `_unwrap_fence`\n38\t\"\"\"\n39\t\n40\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n41\t\n42\timport json\n43\timport logging\n44\timport re\n45\timport subprocess\n46\tfrom collections.abc import Mapping\n47\t\n48\t__all__ = [\n49\t    \"SubagentSubstrateError\",\n50\t    \"claude_cli_transport\",\n51\t]\n52\t\n53\tlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n54\t\n55\t_INNER_FENCE_RE = re.compile(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n```\", re.DOTALL)\n56\t_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SEC = 300\n57\t\n58\t\n59\tclass SubagentSubstrateError(RuntimeError):\n60\t    \"\"\"Raised when claude CLI subprocess substrate fails irrecoverably.\"\"\"\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef _unwrap_fence(text: str) -> str:\n64\t    \"\"\"Extract JSON content from LLM response.\n65\t\n66\t    Order of attempts:\n67\t    1. Fenced block(s) anywhere in the text: ```json...``` — return LAST match\n68\t       (LLMs often add prose preamble + final answer in fence; sometimes\n69\t       multiple fences appear, the last one is typically the actual answer).\n70\t       This subsumes the whole-text-wrap case.\n71\t    2. First balanced top-level {...}: model emits prose then a JSON object\n72\t       without fence → extract via brace-depth scan.\n73\t    3. Return as-is (downstream JSON parse will fail with diagnostic).\n74\t    \"\"\"\n75\t    inner_matches = _INNER_FENCE_RE.findall(text)\n76\t    if inner_matches:\n77\t        last_match = inner_matches[-1]\n78\t        return str(last_match)\n79\t\n80\t    brace_match = _extract_first_json_object(text)\n81\t    if brace_match is not None:\n82\t        return brace_match\n83\t\n84\t    return text\n85\t\n86\t\n87\tdef _extract_first_json_object(text: str) -> str | None:\n88\t    \"\"\"Scan for the first balanced top-level {...} block. None if not found.\n89\t\n90\t    Naive brace-depth counter; ignores braces inside strings to avoid false\n91\t    positives. Sufficient for LLM JSON output which doesn't embed weird\n92\t    string-escape patterns.\n93\t    \"\"\"\n94\t    depth = 0\n95\t    start = -1\n96\t    in_string = False\n97\t    escape = False\n98\t    for i, ch in enumerate(text):\n99\t        if in_string:\n100\t            if escape:\n101\t                escape = False\n102\t            elif ch == \"\\\\\":\n103\t                escape = True\n104\t            elif ch == '\"':\n105\t                in_string = False\n106\t            continue\n107\t        if ch == '\"':\n108\t            in_string = True\n109\t            continue\n110\t        if ch == \"{\":\n111\t            if depth == 0:\n112\t                start = i\n113\t            depth += 1\n114\t        elif ch == \"}\":\n115\t            depth -= 1\n116\t            if depth == 0 and start >= 0:\n117\t                return text[start : i + 1]\n118\t    return None\n119\t\n120\t\n121\tdef _aggregate_input_tokens(usage: Mapping[str, object]) -> int:\n122\t    \"\"\"Sum input + cache_creation + cache_read for cost-tracking parity.\n123\t\n124\t    cache_read is charged at ~10% but conservative aggregation is fine:\n125\t    the adapter's transport — claude CLI subprocess as zero-marginal-cost LLM substrate.\n\nDrop-in replacement for the default `transport` callable in\n`ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter`. Bills against Claude Code subscription\n(parent OAuth/keychain) — no separate ANTHROPIC_API_KEY required.\n\nSource: agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md § \"User insight mid-S38\"\n        + L-S38-2 lesson candidate (subagent dispatch as LLM substrate)\n        + S43b probe 2026-05-01 (research/r-2026-05-01-claude-cli-substrate.md)\n\nMechanism:\n    subprocess.run([\n        \"claude\", \"-p\",\n        \"--model\", <model>,\n        \"--output-format\", \"json\",\n        \"--disable-slash-commands\",\n        \"--system-prompt\", <system_prompt>,\n    ], input=<user_message>, ...)\n\nReturns CLI JSON envelope:\n    {\"result\": \"<model output (often wrapped in ```json fence)>\",\n     \"total_cost_usd\": <float>,\n     \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\", \"output_tokens\",\n               \"cache_creation_input_tokens\", \"cache_read_input_tokens\"},\n     \"is_error\": <bool>, ...}\n\nAdapter signature returns (text, input_tokens, output_tokens). We aggregate\ninput_tokens = input + cache_creation + cache_read (cache_read charged at\n~10% but bundled approximation is sufficient for cost tracking; exact\ntotal_cost_usd is logged separately for audit).\n\nTrade-offs vs anthropic SDK direct:\n- (+) No API key required; bills against subscription\n- (+) Exact cost reported by CLI (logged at log.info)\n- (-) No `temperature` control — CLI uses model defaults\n- (-) Per-call latency 15-30s (subprocess overhead + CLAUDE.md cache creation)\n- (-) Response often wrapped in ```json fence — stripped by `_unwrap_fence`\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport re\nimport subprocess\nfrom collections.abc import Mapping\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"SubagentSubstrateError\",\n    \"claude_cli_transport\",\n]\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_INNER_FENCE_RE = re.compile(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n```\", re.DOTALL)\n_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SEC = 300\n\n\nclass SubagentSubstrateError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when claude CLI subprocess substrate fails irrecoverably.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _unwrap_fence(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Extract JSON content from LLM response.\n\n    Order of attempts:\n    1. Fenced block(s) anywhere in the text: ```json...``` — return LAST match\n       (LLMs often add prose preamble + final answer in fence; sometimes\n       multiple fences appear, the last one is typically the actual answer).\n       This subsumes the whole-text-wrap case.\n    2. First balanced top-level {...}: model emits prose then a JSON object\n       without fence → extract via brace-depth scan.\n    3. Return as-is (downstream JSON parse will fail with diagnostic).\n    \"\"\"\n    inner_matches = _INNER_FENCE_RE.findall(text)\n    if inner_matches:\n        last_match = inner_matches[-1]\n        return str(last_match)\n\n    brace_match = _extract_first_json_object(text)\n    if brace_match is not None:\n        return brace_match\n\n    return text\n\n\ndef _extract_first_json_object(text: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Scan for the first balanced top-level {...} block. None if not found.\n\n    Naive brace-depth counter; ignores braces inside strings to avoid false\n    positives. Sufficient for LLM JSON output which doesn't embed weird\n    string-escape patterns.\n    \"\"\"\n    depth = 0\n    start = -1\n    in_string = False\n    escape = False\n    for i, ch in enumerate(text):\n        if in_string:\n            if escape:\n                escape = False\n            elif ch == \"\\\\\":\n                escape = True\n            elif ch == '\"':\n                in_string = False\n            continue\n        if ch == '\"':\n            in_string = True\n            continue\n        if ch == \"{\":\n            if depth == 0:\n                start = i\n            depth += 1\n        elif ch == \"}\":\n            depth -= 1\n            if depth == 0 and start >= 0:\n                return text[start : i + 1]\n    return None\n\n\ndef _aggregate_input_tokens(usage: Mapping[str, object]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Sum input + cache_creation + cache_read for cost-tracking parity.\n\n    cache_read is charged at ~10% but conservative aggregation is fine:\n    the adapter's CostTrackerPort uses these token counts for budget cap;\n    over-estimating cost is safer than under-estimating.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def _i(k: str) -> int:\n        v = usage.get(k, 0)\n        return int(v) if isinstance(v, (int, float)) else 0\n\n    return _i(\"input_tokens\") + _i(\"cache_creation_input_tokens\") + _i(\"cache_read_input_tokens\")\n\n\ndef claude_cli_transport(\n    model: str,\n    system_prompt: str,\n    user_message: str,\n    temperature: float,  # noqa: ARG001 — accepted for signature compatibility\n) -> tuple[str, int, int]:\n    \"\"\"Call claude CLI in print mode and return (text, input_tokens, output_tokens).\n\n    `temperature` is accepted for signature compatibility but ignored — claude\n    CLI does not expose a temperature flag (uses model defaults).\n    \"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        \"claude\",\n        \"-p\",\n        \"--model\",\n        model,\n        \"--output-format\",\n        \"json\",\n        \"--disable-slash-commands\",\n        \"--system-prompt\",\n        system_prompt,\n    ]\n    try:\n        proc = subprocess.run(\n            cmd,\n            input=user_message,\n            capture_output=True,\n            text=True,\n            encoding=\"utf-8\",  # Windows: avoid cp1252 default that crashes on UTF-8 bytes\n            errors=\"replace\",\n            timeout=_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SEC,\n            check=False,\n        )\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:\n        raise SubagentSubstrateError(f\"claude CLI timeout after {_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SEC}s\") from exc\n    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n        raise SubagentSubstrateError(\"claude CLI not found on PATH\") from exc\n\n    if proc.returncode != 0:\n        raise SubagentSubstrateError(\n            f\"claude CLI exit {proc.returncode}: {proc.stderr[:500]}\"\n        )\n\n    try:\n        envelope = json.loads(proc.stdout)\n    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n        raise SubagentSubstrateError(\n            f\"claude CLI returned non-JSON: {proc.stdout[:500]}\"\n        ) from exc\n\n    if envelope.get(\"is_error\"):\n        raise SubagentSubstrateError(\n            f\"claude CLI api_error_status={envelope.get('api_error_status')} \"\n            f\"result={str(envelope.get('result'))[:200]}\"\n        )\n\n    text = _unwrap_fence(str(envelope.get(\"result\", \"\")))\n    usage = envelope.get(\"usage\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(usage, dict):\n        usage = {}\n\n    in_tok = _aggregate_input_tokens(usage)\n    out_tok_v = usage.get(\"output_tokens\", 0)\n    out_tok = int(out_tok_v) if isinstance(out_tok_v, (int, float)) else 0\n    cli_cost = envelope.get(\"total_cost_usd\")\n    log.info(\n        \"subagent_transport: model=%s in_tok=%d out_tok=%d cli_cost_usd=%s\",\n        model,\n        in_tok,\n        out_tok,\n        cli_cost,\n    )\n    return text, in_tok, out_tok\n","numLines":209,"startLine":1,"totalLines":209}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9f1e82af-0b27-4dc9-ab76-6823c9d97f69","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

noqa: PLC0415\n            compute_ta_features,\n        )\n        from packages.infrastructure.fundamental.sqlite_fundamental_repository import (  # noqa: PLC0415\n            SqliteFundamentalRepository,\n        )\n        from packages.infrastructure.market_data.sqlite_bar_repository import (  # noqa: PLC0415\n            SqliteBarRepository,\n        )\n        from packages.infrastructure.news.sqlite_news_repository import (  # noqa: PLC0415\n            SqliteClaimRepository,\n            SqliteNewsRepository,\n        )\n\n        _ticker = ticker if isinstance(ticker, _Ticker) else _Ticker(str(ticker))\n        _as_of = as_of if isinstance(as_of, _date) else _date.today()\n        start_90 = _as_of - timedelta(days=90)\n\n        bar_repo = SqliteBarRepository(db_path=self._db_path)\n        fund_repo = SqliteFundamentalRepository(db_path=self._db_path)\n        news_repo = SqliteNewsRepository(db_path=self._db_path)\n        claim_repo = SqliteClaimRepository(db_path=self._db_path)\n\n        bars = bar_repo.get_as_of(_ticker, _as_of)\n        statements_all = fund_repo.get_as_of(_ticker, _as_of)\n\n        # Latest close → price_per_share for P/E + P/B (S43b-EVIDENCE: was None\n        # in S43b-DATA \"Phase 2 thin slice\"; now passed so Quant has multiples).\n        latest_close = getattr(bars[-1], \"close\", None) if bars else None\n\n        # Compute ratios from latest 4 IS quarters + latest BS\n        income_stmts = [s for s in statements_all if s.statement_type == StatementType.IS][-4:]\n        bs_stmts = [s for s in statements_all if s.statement_type == StatementType.BS]\n        latest_bs = bs_stmts[-1] if bs_stmts else None\n        ratios_ttm: dict[str, object] = {}\n        if income_stmts and latest_bs is not None:\n            try:\n                rs_result = RatioService().compute_ttm_ratios(\n                    ticker=_ticker,\n                    period_end=_as_of,\n                    income_statements=income_stmts,\n                    latest_balance_sheet=latest_bs,\n                    price_per_share=latest_close,  # S43b-EVIDENCE: enables P/E + P/B\n                )\n                ratios_ttm = {str(k): v for k, v in rs_result.items()}\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — log + continue per Q-S28-3\n                ratios_ttm = {}\n\n        # TA features (S43b-EVIDENCE; deterministic per I-S1; LLM only interprets)\n        ta = compute_ta_features(bars)\n        ta_features = ta.to_dict()\n        ta_audit = ta.audit\n\n        # News: fetch all-known-as-of, filter to 90d window\n        all_news = news_repo.get_known_as_of(_ticker, _as_of)\n        recent_news: list[object] = [\n            n for n in all_news\n            if getattr(n, \"published_at\", None) is not None\n            and n.published_at.date() >= start_90\n        ]\n        recent_claims = claim_repo.get_for_ticker(_ticker, _as_of)\n\n        gaps: list[str] = []\n        if not bars or (_as_of - bars[-1].period_end).days > 3:\n            gaps.append(\"price_stale\")\n        if not statements_all:\n            gaps.append(\"fundamentals_stale\")\n        if not recent_news:\n            gaps.append(\"no_news_90d\")\n\n        # Deterministic snapshot md5\n        payload = _json.dumps(\n            {\n                \"bars\": sorted(b.bar_id for b in bars),\n                \"statements\": sorted(str(s.period_end) + \"_\" + str(s.statement_type) for s in statements_all),\n                \"news\": sorted(getattr(n, \"article_id\", \"\") for n in recent_news),\n                \"claims\": sorted(getattr(c, \"claim_id\", \"\") for c in recent_claims),\n            },\n            sort_keys=True,\n        )\n        data_md5 = hashlib.md5(payload.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()\n\n        # SharedContext.statements expects a single object (latest statement) per\n        # claude_llm_perspective_adapter._context_to_str access pattern. Pass\n        # latest_bs (closest to balance-sheet view) when available.\n        return SharedContext(\n            ticker=_ticker,\n            as_of=_as_of,\n            quotes=list(bars),\n            statements=latest_bs,\n            ratios_ttm=ratios_ttm,\n            recent_news=recent_news,\n            recent_claims=list(recent_claims),\n            ta_features=ta_features,\n            ta_audit=ta_audit,\n            gaps=gaps,\n            data_snapshot_md5=data_md5,\n        )\n","numLines":428,"startLine":1,"totalLines":428}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"080ee689-c876-4805-8693-d4f20aeb8996","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

This adapter only COMPUTES cost_usd per call.\n21\t\n22\tSource: specs/tier2-feature/006-phase-2-track-F-thesis-pipeline.md § B.10 + § B.11.\n23\t\"\"\"\n24\t\n25\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n26\t\n27\timport hashlib\n28\timport json\n29\timport logging\n30\tfrom collections.abc import Callable\n31\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n32\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n33\t\n34\tfrom packages.domain.analysis.models.perspective_analysis import PerspectiveRole\n35\t\n36\t__all__ = [\"ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter\"]\n37\t\n38\tlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n39\t\n40\t# Model IDs per spec § B.10 (+ Haiku for per-role override per S43b-BULL DEFER-S43b-3 fix)\n41\t_SONNET_MODEL = \"claude-sonnet-4-6\"\n42\t_OPUS_MODEL = \"claude-opus-4-7\"\n43\t_HAIKU_MODEL = \"claude-haiku-4-5\"\n44\t\n45\t# Cost per million tokens (USD) — spec § B.10 / A.10 data provenance.\n46\t# Haiku rate: Anthropic public pricing (Haiku 4.5 = $1.00 in / $5.00 out per MTok).\n47\t# In subagent transport path, CLI-reported total_cost_usd is the source of truth\n48\t# (logged at log.info); these rates are used when SDK transport returns raw token counts.\n49\t_COST_PER_MTOK: dict[str, dict[str, Decimal]] = {\n50\t    _SONNET_MODEL: {\n51\t        \"input\": Decimal(\"3.00\"),\n52\t        \"output\": Decimal(\"15.00\"),\n53\t    },\n54\t    _OPUS_MODEL: {\n55\t        \"input\": Decimal(\"15.00\"),\n56\t        \"output\": Decimal(\"75.00\"),\n57\t    },\n58\t    _HAIKU_MODEL: {\n59\t        \"input\": Decimal(\"1.00\"),\n60\t        \"output\": Decimal(\"5.00\"),\n61\t    },\n62\t}\n63\t\n64\t# Role → model routing per spec § B.10\n65\t_ROLE_TO_MODEL: dict[PerspectiveRole, str] = {\n66\t    PerspectiveRole.BEAR: _SONNET_MODEL,\n67\t    PerspectiveRole.BULL: _SONNET_MODEL,\n68\t    PerspectiveRole.QUANT: _OPUS_MODEL,\n69\t}\n70\t_DEFAULT_MODEL = _OPUS_MODEL  # synthesizer and fallback\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef _default_transport(\n74\t    model: str, system_prompt: str, user_message: str, temperature: float\n75\t) -> tuple[str, int, int]:\n76\t    \"\"\"Default Anthropic SDK call. Lazily imported — not needed in tests.\n77\t\n78\t    Returns (response_text, input_tokens, output_tokens).\n79\t    \"\"\"\n80\t    import anthropic  # type: ignore[import-not-found]\n81\t\n82\t    client = anthropic.Anthropic()\n83\t    response = client.messages.create(\n84\t        model=model,\n85\t        max_tokens=4096,\n86\t        temperature=temperature,\n87\t        system=system_prompt,\n88\t        messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user_message}],\n89\t    )\n90\t    text_parts: list[str] = []\n91\t    for block in response.content:\n92\t        text = getattr(block, \"text\", None)\n93\t        if isinstance(text, str):\n94\t            text_parts.append(text)\n95\t    usage = getattr(response, \"usage\", None)\n96\t    input_tokens = getattr(usage, \"input_tokens\", 0) if usage else 0\n97\t    output_tokens = getattr(usage, \"output_tokens\", 0) if usage else 0\n98\t    return \"\".join(text_parts), input_tokens, output_tokens\n99\t\n100\t\n101\tdef _compute_cost(model: str, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int) -> Decimal:\n102\t    \"\"\"Deterministic cost computation from token counts. No LLM math — pure arithmetic.\"\"\"\n103\t    rates = _COST_PER_MTOK.get(model, _COST_PER_MTOK[_DEFAULT_MODEL])\n104\t    cost = (\n105\t        rates[\"input\"] * Decimal(input_tokens) / Decimal(\"1_000_000\")\n106\t        + rates[\"output\"] * Decimal(output_tokens) / Decimal(\"1_000_000\")\n107\t    )\n108\t    return cost\n109\t\n110\t\n111\tdef _context_to_str(context: object) -> str:\n112\t    \"\"\"Convert SharedContext to a user message string for the LLM.\n113\t\n114\t    Only includes data that LLM should interpret — never produces numbers itself.\n115\t    Numbers from ratios_ttm are passed verbatim (already code-computed).\n116\t    \"\"\"\n117\t    parts: list[str] = []\n118\t\n119\t    ticker_symbol = str(getattr(getattr(context, \"ticker\", None), \"symbol\", \"UNKNOWN\"))\n120\t    as_of = str(getattr(context, \"as_of\", \"\"))\n121\t    parts.append(f\"=== SharedContext for {ticker_symbol} as_of={as_of} ===\\n\")\n122\t\n123\t    quotes = getattr(context, \"quotes\", [])\n124\t    if quotes:\n125\t        parts.append(f\"[Bar History] {len(quotes)} bars loaded.\\n\")\n126\t        # Include last bar details safely\n127\t        last_bar = quotes[-1]\n128\t        last_close = getattr(last_bar, \"close\", None)\n129\t        last_date = getattr(last_bar, \"period_end\", None)\n130\t        if last_close is not None:\n131\t            parts.append(f\"  Latest close: {last_close} as of {last_date}\\n\")\n132\t\n133\t    statements = getattr(context, \"statements\", None)\n134\t    if statements is not None:\n135\t        filing_date = getattr(statements, \"filing_date\", None)\n136\t        parts.append(f\"[Fundamentals] Filing date: {filing_date}\\n\")\n137\t\n138\t    ratios = getattr(context, \"ratios_ttm\", {})\n139\t    if ratios:\n140\t        parts.append(\"[TTM Ratios — computed by code; interpret only]\\n\")\n141\t        for name, ratio in ratios.items():\n142\t            val = getattr(ratio, \"value\", ratio)\n143\t            audit = getattr(ratio, \"formula_audit\", \"\")\n144\t            parts.append(f\"  {name}: {val}  -- formula: {audit}\\n\")\n145\t\n146\t    ta_features = getattr(context, \"ta_features\", {})\n147\t    ta_audit = getattr(context, \"ta_audit\", {})\n148\t    if ta_features:\n149\t        parts.append(\"[TA Features — computed by code; interpret only]\\n\")\n150\t        for name, value in ta_features.items():\n151\t            audit_key = \"sma_N\" if str(name).startswith(\"sma_\") else str(name)\n152\t            audit = ta_audit.get(audit_key, \"\") if isinstance(ta_audit, dict) else \"\"\n153\t            parts.append(f\"  {name}: {value}  -- formula: {audit}\\n\")\n154\t\n155\t    news = getattr(context, \"recent_news\", [])\n156\t    claims = getattr(context, \"recent_claims\", [])\n157\t    parts.append(f\"[News] {len(news)} articles / {len(claims)} claims in last 90 days.\\n\")\n158\t    for claim in claims[:10]:  # top 10 claims\n159\t        text = getattr(claim, \"claim_text\", \"\")\n160\t        url = getattr(claim, \"source_url\", \"\")\n161\t        excerpt = getattr(claim, \"source_text_excerpt\", \"\")\n162\t        sentiment = str(getattr(claim, \"sentiment\", \"\"))\n163\t        parts.append(f\"  CLAIM [{sentiment}]: {text[:200]}\\n  SOURCE: {url}\\n  EXCERPT: {excerpt[:200]}\\n\")\n164\t    # When claims are absent (extraction not yet run), surface news articles\n165\t    # directly so the LLM has grounded source_url + body_excerpt to cite.\n166\t    if not claims:\n167\t        for art in news[:10]:\n168\t            title = getattr(art, \"title\", \"\")\n169\t            url = getattr(art, \"source_url\", \"\")\n170\t            excerpt = getattr(art, \"body_excerpt\", \"\")\n171\t            published = getattr(art, \"published_at\", \"\")\n172\t            parts.append(\n173\t                f\"  NEWS: {title[:160]}\\n  SOURCE: {url}\\n  PUBLISHED: {published}\\n  EXCERPT: {excerpt[:300]}\\n\"\n174\t            )\n175\t\n176\t    gaps = getattr(context, \"gaps\", [])\n177\t    if gaps:\n178\t        parts.append(f\"[Data Gaps] {gaps}\\n\")\n179\t\n180\t    return \"\".join(parts)\n181\t\n182\t\n183\t@dataclass\n184\tclass ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter:\n185\t    \"\"\"Anthropic SDK wrapper for perspective agent LLM calls.\n186\t\n187\t    `transport`: pluggable (model, system_prompt, user_message, temperature) -> (text, in_tok, out_tok).\n188\t    Default uses anthropic.Anthropic lazily. Tests inject a closure.\n189\t    `model_override`: global override for all calls (e.g. forcing one model in tests).\n190\t    `role_model_overrides`: per-role override applied when `model_override` is None.\n191\t        Used by S43b-BULL fix (DEFER-S43b-3): map BULL → haiku to side-step the\n192\t        300s sonnet timeout that empirically reproduces 2/2 dogfood runs.\n193\t        Resolution order (first wins): model_override → role_model_overrides[role]\n194\t        → _ROLE_TO_MODEL[role] → _DEFAULT_MODEL.\n195\t    \"\"\"\n196\t\n197\t    transport: Callable[[str, str, str, float], tuple[str, int, int]] = field(\n198\t        default=_default_transport\n199\t    )\n200\t    model_override: str | None = None\n201\t    role_model_overrides: dict[PerspectiveRole, str] | None = None\n202\t    temperature: float = 0.0  # AC-5 reproducibility\n203\t\n204\t    async def call_llm(\n205\t        self,\n206\t        system_prompt: str,\n207\t        context: object,\n208\t        role: PerspectiveRole,\n209\t    ) -> tuple[str, Decimal, str, str]:\n210\t        \"\"\"Call the LLM with role-appropriate model and return structured output.\n211\t\n212\t        Returns (raw_json_str, cost_usd, model_id, prompt_hash).\n213\t        raw_json_str is expected to be a JSON string matching PerspectiveAnalysis schema.\n214\t        \"\"\"\n215\t        model = (\n216\t            self.model_override\n217\t            or (self.role_model_overrides or {}).get(role)\n218\t            or _ROLE_TO_MODEL.get(role, _DEFAULT_MODEL)\n219\t        )\n220\t        user_message = _context_to_str(context)\n221\t        prompt_hash = hashlib.sha256(system_prompt.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:16]\n222\t\n223\t        try:\n224\t            raw_text, input_tokens, output_tokens = self.transport(\n225\t                model, system_prompt, user_message, self.temperature\n226\t            )\n227\t        except Exception as exc:\n228\t            log.error(\"LLM call failed for role=%s model=%s: %s\", role, model, exc)\n229\t            # Return empty key_points JSON — caller handles insufficient case\n230\t            return (\n231\t                json.dumps({\"key_points\": []}),\n232\t                Decimal(\"0\"),\n233\t                model,\n234\t                prompt_hash,\n235\t            )\n236\t\n237\t        cost_usd = _compute_cost(model, input_tokens, output_tokens)\n238\t\n239\t        # Validate response is parseable JSON\n240\t        try:\n241\t            parsed = json.loads(raw_text)\n242\t            if not isinstance(parsed, dict):\n243\t                log.warning(\n244\t                    \"LLM response not a dict for role=%s; wrapping. Snippet: %s\",\n245\t                    role, raw_text[:300],\n246\t                )\n247\t                raw_text = json.dumps({\"key_points\": []})\n248\t        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n249\t            log.warning(\n250\t                \"LLM response not valid JSON for role=%s (%s); defaulting empty. \"\n251\t                \"Snippet (first 600 chars): %s\",\n252\t                role, exc, raw_text[:600],\n253\t            )\n254\t            raw_text = json.dumps({\"key_points\": []})\n255\t\n256\t        return raw_text, cost_usd, model, prompt_hash\n257\t"}]},"uuid":"110f6567-6767-4d6c-8ce8-679da7309942","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:01:21.961Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\infrastructure\\analysis\\claude_llm_perspective_adapter.py","content":"\"\"\"ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter — Anthropic SDK wrapper for perspective agents.\n\nPattern reused from packages/infrastructure/news/claude_llm_extractor.py (S36).\n\nKey features:\n- temperature=0.0 pinned for reproducibility (AC-5)\n- prompt_hash: sha256[:16] of system prompt (reproducibility audit)\n- cost_usd: computed from input + output token counts (Decimal; never float)\n- Lazy Anthropic SDK import: tests inject transport callable → no network in CI\n- context_to_str: converts SharedContext to a user message string\n\nLLM call signature:\n  call_llm(system_prompt, context, role) -> (raw_json_str, cost_usd, model_id, prompt_hash)\n\nModel routing per spec § B.10:\n- Bear/Bull: claude-sonnet-4-6 ($3/MTok in + $15/MTok out)\n- Quant/Synthesizer: claude-opus-4-7 ($15/MTok in + $75/MTok out)\n\nCostBudgetExceeded is not raised here — cost tracking is the use case's job via\nCostTrackerPort. This adapter only COMPUTES cost_usd per call.\n\nSource: specs/tier2-feature/006-phase-2-track-F-thesis-pipeline.md § B.10 + § B.11.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom collections.abc import Callable\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom packages.domain.analysis.models.perspective_analysis import PerspectiveRole\n\n__all__ = [\"ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter\"]\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Model IDs per spec § B.10 (+ Haiku for per-role override per S43b-BULL DEFER-S43b-3 fix)\n_SONNET_MODEL = \"claude-sonnet-4-6\"\n_OPUS_MODEL = \"claude-opus-4-7\"\n_HAIKU_MODEL = \"claude-haiku-4-5\"\n\n# Cost per million tokens (USD) — spec § B.10 / A.10 data provenance.\n# Haiku rate: Anthropic public pricing (Haiku 4.5 = $1.00 in / $5.00 out per MTok).\n# In subagent transport path, CLI-reported total_cost_usd is the source of truth\n# (logged at log.info); these rates are used when SDK transport returns raw token counts.\n_COST_PER_MTOK: dict[str, dict[str, Decimal]] = {\n    _SONNET_MODEL: {\n        \"input\": Decimal(\"3.00\"),\n        \"output\": Decimal(\"15.00\"),\n    },\n    _OPUS_MODEL: {\n        \"input\": Decimal(\"15.00\"),\n        \"output\": Decimal(\"75.00\"),\n    },\n    _HAIKU_MODEL: {\n        \"input\": Decimal(\"1.00\"),\n        \"output\": Decimal(\"5.00\"),\n    },\n}\n\n# Role → model routing per spec § B.10\n_ROLE_TO_MODEL: dict[PerspectiveRole, str] = {\n    PerspectiveRole.BEAR: _SONNET_MODEL,\n    PerspectiveRole.BULL: _SONNET_MODEL,\n    PerspectiveRole.QUANT: _OPUS_MODEL,\n}\n_DEFAULT_MODEL = _OPUS_MODEL  # synthesizer and fallback\n\n\ndef _default_transport(\n    model: str, system_prompt: str, user_message: str, temperature: float\n) -> tuple[str, int, int]:\n    \"\"\"Default Anthropic SDK call. Lazily imported — not needed in tests.\n\n    Returns (response_text, input_tokens, output_tokens).\n    \"\"\"\n    import anthropic  # type: ignore[import-not-found]\n\n    client = anthropic.Anthropic()\n    response = client.messages.create(\n        model=model,\n        max_tokens=4096,\n        temperature=temperature,\n        system=system_prompt,\n        messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user_message}],\n    )\n    text_parts: list[str] = []\n    for block in response.content:\n        text = getattr(block, \"text\", None)\n        if isinstance(text, str):\n            text_parts.append(text)\n    usage = getattr(response, \"usage\", None)\n    input_tokens = getattr(usage, \"input_tokens\", 0) if usage else 0\n    output_tokens = getattr(usage, \"output_tokens\", 0) if usage else 0\n    return \"\".join(text_parts), input_tokens, output_tokens\n\n\ndef _compute_cost(model: str, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int) -> Decimal:\n    \"\"\"Deterministic cost computation from token counts. No LLM math — pure arithmetic.\"\"\"\n    rates = _COST_PER_MTOK.get(model, _COST_PER_MTOK[_DEFAULT_MODEL])\n    cost = (\n        rates[\"input\"] * Decimal(input_tokens) / Decimal(\"1_000_000\")\n        + rates[\"output\"] * Decimal(output_tokens) / Decimal(\"1_000_000\")\n    )\n    return cost\n\n\ndef _context_to_str(context: object) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Convert SharedContext to a user message string for the LLM.\n\n    Only includes data that LLM should interpret — never produces numbers itself.\n    Numbers from ratios_ttm are passed verbatim (already code-computed).\n    \"\"\"\n    parts: list[str] = []\n\n    ticker_symbol = str(getattr(getattr(context, \"ticker\", None), \"symbol\", \"UNKNOWN\"))\n    as_of = str(getattr(context, \"as_of\", \"\"))\n    parts.append(f\"=== SharedContext for {ticker_symbol} as_of={as_of} ===\\n\")\n\n    quotes = getattr(context, \"quotes\", [])\n    if quotes:\n        parts.append(f\"[Bar History] {len(quotes)} bars loaded.\\n\")\n        # Include last bar details safely\n        last_bar = quotes[-1]\n        last_close = getattr(last_bar, \"close\", None)\n        last_date = getattr(last_bar, \"period_end\", None)\n        if last_close is not None:\n            parts.append(f\"  Latest close: {last_close} as of {last_date}\\n\")\n\n    statements = getattr(context, \"statements\", None)\n    if statements is not None:\n        filing_date = getattr(statements, \"filing_date\", None)\n        parts.append(f\"[Fundamentals] Filing date: {filing_date}\\n\")\n\n    ratios = getattr(context, \"ratios_ttm\", {})\n    if ratios:\n        parts.append(\"[TTM Ratios — computed by code; interpret only]\\n\")\n        for name, ratio in ratios.items():\n            val = getattr(ratio, \"value\", ratio)\n            audit = getattr(ratio, \"formula_audit\", \"\")\n            parts.append(f\"  {name}: {val}  -- formula: {audit}\\n\")\n\n    ta_features = getattr(context, \"ta_features\", {})\n    ta_audit = getattr(context, \"ta_audit\", {})\n    if ta_features:\n        parts.append(\"[TA Features — computed by code; interpret only]\\n\")\n        for name, value in ta_features.items():\n            audit_key = \"sma_N\" if str(name).startswith(\"sma_\") else str(name)\n            audit = ta_audit.get(audit_key, \"\") if isinstance(ta_audit, dict) else \"\"\n            parts.append(f\"  {name}: {value}  -- formula: {audit}\\n\")\n\n    news = getattr(context, \"recent_news\", [])\n    claims = getattr(context, \"recent_claims\", [])\n    parts.append(f\"[News] {len(news)} articles / {len(claims)} claims in last 90 days.\\n\")\n    for claim in claims[:10]:  # top 10 claims\n        text = getattr(claim, \"claim_text\", \"\")\n        url = getattr(claim, \"source_url\", \"\")\n        excerpt = getattr(claim, \"source_text_excerpt\", \"\")\n        sentiment = str(getattr(claim, \"sentiment\", \"\"))\n        parts.append(f\"  CLAIM [{sentiment}]: {text[:200]}\\n  SOURCE: {url}\\n  EXCERPT: {excerpt[:200]}\\n\")\n    # When claims are absent (extraction not yet run), surface news articles\n    # directly so the LLM has grounded source_url + body_excerpt to cite.\n    if not claims:\n        for art in news[:10]:\n            title = getattr(art, \"title\", \"\")\n            url = getattr(art, \"source_url\", \"\")\n            excerpt = getattr(art, \"body_excerpt\", \"\")\n            published = getattr(art, \"published_at\", \"\")\n            parts.append(\n                f\"  NEWS: {title[:160]}\\n  SOURCE: {url}\\n  PUBLISHED: {published}\\n  EXCERPT: {excerpt[:300]}\\n\"\n            )\n\n    gaps = getattr(context, \"gaps\", [])\n    if gaps:\n        parts.append(f\"[Data Gaps] {gaps}\\n\")\n\n    return \"\".join(parts)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter:\n    \"\"\"Anthropic SDK wrapper for perspective agent LLM calls.\n\n    `transport`: pluggable (model, system_prompt, user_message, temperature) -> (text, in_tok, out_tok).\n    Default uses anthropic.Anthropic lazily. Tests inject a closure.\n    `model_override`: global override for all calls (e.g. forcing one model in tests).\n    `role_model_overrides`: per-role override applied when `model_override` is None.\n        Used by S43b-BULL fix (DEFER-S43b-3): map BULL → haiku to side-step the\n        300s sonnet timeout that empirically reproduces 2/2 dogfood runs.\n        Resolution order (first wins): model_override → role_model_overrides[role]\n        → _ROLE_TO_MODEL[role] → _DEFAULT_MODEL.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    transport: Callable[[str, str, str, float], tuple[str, int, int]] = field(\n        default=_default_transport\n    )\n    model_override: str | None = None\n    role_model_overrides: dict[PerspectiveRole, str] | None = None\n    temperature: float = 0.0  # AC-5 reproducibility\n\n    async def call_llm(\n        self,\n        system_prompt: str,\n        context: object,\n        role: PerspectiveRole,\n    ) -> tuple[str, Decimal, str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Call the LLM with role-appropriate model and return structured output.\n\n        Returns (raw_json_str, cost_usd, model_id, prompt_hash).\n        raw_json_str is expected to be a JSON string matching PerspectiveAnalysis schema.\n        \"\"\"\n        model = (\n            self.model_override\n            or (self.role_model_overrides or {}).get(role)\n            or _ROLE_TO_MODEL.get(role, _DEFAULT_MODEL)\n        )\n        user_message = _context_to_str(context)\n        prompt_hash = hashlib.sha256(system_prompt.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:16]\n\n        try:\n            raw_text, input_tokens, output_tokens = self.transport(\n                model, system_prompt, user_message, self.temperature\n            )\n        except Exception as exc:\n            log.error(\"LLM call failed for role=%s model=%s: %s\", role, model, exc)\n            # Return empty key_points JSON — caller handles insufficient case\n            return (\n                json.dumps({\"key_points\": []}),\n                Decimal(\"0\"),\n                model,\n                prompt_hash,\n            )\n\n        cost_usd = _compute_cost(model, input_tokens, output_tokens)\n\n        # Validate response is parseable JSON\n        try:\n            parsed = json.loads(raw_text)\n            if not isinstance(parsed, dict):\n                log.warning(\n                    \"LLM response not a dict for role=%s; wrapping. Snippet: %s\",\n                    role, raw_text[:300],\n                )\n                raw_text = json.dumps({\"key_points\": []})\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n            log.warning(\n                \"LLM response not valid JSON for role=%s (%s); defaulting empty. \"\n                \"Snippet (first 600 chars): %s\",\n                role, exc, raw_text[:600],\n            )\n            raw_text = json.dumps({\"key_points\": []})\n\n        return raw_text, cost_usd, model, prompt_hash\n","numLines":257,"startLine":1,"totalLines":257}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e9c4b028-f770-406c-8fcb-43df20200412","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
