You MUST follow these\n59\trules absolutely:\n60\t\n61\tRULE 1 — NO LLM MATH. Do not produce numeric scores, do not estimate price\n62\timpact, do not aggregate. Sentiment is one of five categorical labels:\n63\tstrongly_bullish | bullish | neutral | bearish | strongly_bearish.\n64\t\n65\tRULE 2 — GROUNDING. Every claim must include a `source_text_excerpt`: a\n66\tverbatim quote from the article body (≤500 characters) that supports the\n67\tclaim. If you cannot quote the article verbatim, do NOT emit the claim.\n68\t\n69\tRULE 3 — ENTITY GROUNDING. Every claim must mention at least one ticker\n70\t(uppercase 3-letter VN symbol) or sector. Claims with no entities are noise\n71\tand must be omitted.\n72\t\n73\tRULE 4 — OUTPUT. Return ONLY a JSON object of the form\n74\t`{\"claims\": [...]}`. No prose, no markdown fences, no preamble.\n75\t\"\"\"\n76\t\n77\t_PROMPT_HASH = hashlib.sha256(_SYSTEM_PROMPT.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:16]\n78\t\n79\t\n80\tdef _default_transport(system_prompt: str, body: str) -> str:\n81\t    \"\"\"Default Anthropic SDK call. Imported lazily so anthropic is not a\n82\t    test-time requirement when a transport stub is injected.\n83\t    \"\"\"\n84\t    import anthropic  # type: ignore[import-not-found]\n85\t\n86\t    client = anthropic.Anthropic()\n87\t    response = client.messages.create(\n88\t        model=_DEFAULT_MODEL,\n89\t        max_tokens=2048,\n90\t        system=system_prompt,\n91\t        messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": body}],\n92\t    )\n93\t    blocks = response.content\n94\t    text_parts: list[str] = []\n95\t    for block in blocks:\n96\t        text = getattr(block, \"text\", None)\n97\t        if isinstance(text, str):\n98\t            text_parts.append(text)\n99\t    return \"\".join(text_parts)\n100\t\n101\t\n102\t@dataclass\n103\tclass ClaudeLlmExtractor:\n104\t    \"\"\"LlmExtractorPort adapter backed by the Anthropic SDK.\n105\t\n106\t    `transport`: pluggable string-in / string-out call (system prompt + body\n107\t    → raw response text). The default uses anthropic.Anthropic; tests\n108\t    inject a closure over recorded responses.\n109\t    `clock`: time source for ExtractorMetadata.extracted_at.\n110\t    \"\"\"\n111\t\n112\t    transport: Callable[[str, str], str] = _default_transport\n113\t    clock: Callable[[], datetime] = field(\n114\t        default_factory=lambda: lambda: datetime.now(UTC)\n115\t    )\n116\t    model: str = _DEFAULT_MODEL\n117\t    version: str = _DEFAULT_VERSION\n118\t    system_prompt: str = _SYSTEM_PROMPT\n119\t\n120\t    def extract(self, article: NewsArticle) -> list[ExtractedClaim]:"}]},"uuid":"e163e83d-49a1-4a5b-8dca-ec7c2b3ad2a2","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:01:51.622Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\infrastructure\\news\\claude_llm_extractor.py","content":"\"\"\"ClaudeLlmExtractor — Anthropic SDK adapter implementing LlmExtractorPort.\n\nPhase 2 thin slice: Sonnet (not Opus) per master-plan 005 § S36 cost cap\n(≤$0.10/article); production calls use prompt caching on the system prompt\nto amortise cost across an ingestion batch (skill: claude-api).\n\nThe adapter is fixture-friendly: tests inject a `transport` callable\n(prompt: str, body: str) -> str (raw LLM JSON response) so CI never touches\nthe network. The default transport calls `anthropic.Anthropic().messages.create`\nlazily — anthropic SDK is imported only when an adapter without an injected\ntransport actually runs.\n\nReturned JSON contract (system prompt enforces; adapter validates):\n```\n{\n  \"claims\": [\n    {\n      \"claim_text\": str,\n      \"source_text_excerpt\": str (≤500 chars verbatim quote),\n      \"sentiment\": \"strongly_bullish\" | \"bullish\" | \"neutral\"\n                  | \"bearish\" | \"strongly_bearish\",\n      \"mentioned_tickers\": [str, ...],\n      \"mentioned_sectors\": [str, ...],\n      \"key_phrases\": [str, ...],\n      \"tone_indicators\": [str, ...],\n      \"confidence\": float [0, 1]\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\nSource: financial-data-protocol.md Rule 6 + Rule 7;\nspecs/tier1-strategic/001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md § B.2;\n.claude/skills/claude-api/SKILL.md.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nfrom collections.abc import Callable\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom datetime import UTC, datetime\n\nfrom packages.contracts import Ticker\nfrom packages.domain.news.models import ExtractedClaim, NewsArticle\nfrom packages.domain.news.value_objects import ExtractorMetadata, Sentiment\n\n__all__ = [\"ClaudeLlmExtractor\"]\n\n\n_DEFAULT_MODEL = \"claude-sonnet-4-6\"\n_DEFAULT_VERSION = \"stockforge-news-extractor-0.1\"\n\n_SYSTEM_PROMPT = \"\"\"You are a Vietnamese stock-market news claim extractor.\n\nYour job is to read one Vietnamese financial-news article and emit a JSON\nobject describing the structured claims it contains. You MUST follow these\nrules absolutely:\n\nRULE 1 — NO LLM MATH. Do not produce numeric scores, do not estimate price\nimpact, do not aggregate. Sentiment is one of five categorical labels:\nstrongly_bullish | bullish | neutral | bearish | strongly_bearish.\n\nRULE 2 — GROUNDING. Every claim must include a `source_text_excerpt`: a\nverbatim quote from the article body (≤500 characters) that supports the\nclaim. If you cannot quote the article verbatim, do NOT emit the claim.\n\nRULE 3 — ENTITY GROUNDING. Every claim must mention at least one ticker\n(uppercase 3-letter VN symbol) or sector. Claims with no entities are noise\nand must be omitted.\n\nRULE 4 — OUTPUT. Return ONLY a JSON object of the form\n`{\"claims\": [...]}`. No prose, no markdown fences, no preamble.\n\"\"\"\n\n_PROMPT_HASH = hashlib.sha256(_SYSTEM_PROMPT.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:16]\n\n\ndef _default_transport(system_prompt: str, body: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Default Anthropic SDK call. Imported lazily so anthropic is not a\n    test-time requirement when a transport stub is injected.\n    \"\"\"\n    import anthropic  # type: ignore[import-not-found]\n\n    client = anthropic.Anthropic()\n    response = client.messages.create(\n        model=_DEFAULT_MODEL,\n        max_tokens=2048,\n        system=system_prompt,\n        messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": body}],\n    )\n    blocks = response.content\n    text_parts: list[str] = []\n    for block in blocks:\n        text = getattr(block, \"text\", None)\n        if isinstance(text, str):\n            text_parts.append(text)\n    return \"\".join(text_parts)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ClaudeLlmExtractor:\n    \"\"\"LlmExtractorPort adapter backed by the Anthropic SDK.\n\n    `transport`: pluggable string-in / string-out call (system prompt + body\n    → raw response text). The default uses anthropic.Anthropic; tests\n    inject a closure over recorded responses.\n    `clock`: time source for ExtractorMetadata.extracted_at.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    transport: Callable[[str, str], str] = _default_transport\n    clock: Callable[[], datetime] = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: lambda: datetime.now(UTC)\n    )\n    model: str = _DEFAULT_MODEL\n    version: str = _DEFAULT_VERSION\n    system_prompt: str = _SYSTEM_PROMPT\n\n    def extract(self, article: NewsArticle) -> list[ExtractedClaim]:","numLines":120,"startLine":1,"totalLines":226}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bd869fcb-fe56-44e3-bb99-96194ead0c1c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

— CLI for running a 3-perspective thesis analysis.\n2\t\n3\tUsage:\n4\t    python apps/cli/validate_thesis.py --ticker HPG --as-of 2026-04-29\n5\t    python apps/cli/validate_thesis.py --ticker HPG --mock-llm\n6\t\n7\tOn success (exit 0): thesis written to {output_dir}/{as_of}-{ticker}.md\n8\tOn CostBudgetExceeded (exit 1): prints error + partial cost.\n9\tOn BearCaseInvariantError / INCOMPLETE thesis (exit 2): prints error.\n10\tOn live-LLM gate not resolved (exit 3): NotImplementedError printed.\n11\t\n12\tHARD BINDING:\n13\t- --mock-llm injects MockLLMPerspectivePort (no Anthropic call; safe for CI).\n14\t- --no-mock-llm checks ANTHROPIC_API_KEY; absent → exit with helpful error (exit 4).\n15\t  ANTHROPIC_API_KEY present → NotImplementedError (S43a-DOGFOOD gate; exit 3).\n16\t- NEVER makes a live Anthropic call in S43a-CODE.\n17\t\n18\tI-S35: every output markdown includes disclaimer footer.\n19\tUTF-8 everywhere; Vietnamese diacritics preserved.\n20\t\n21\tSource: specs/tier2-feature/006-phase-2-track-F-thesis-pipeline.md § B.7 (UC-2)\n22\t\"\"\"\n23\t\n24\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n25\t\n26\timport asyncio\n27\timport sys\n28\tfrom datetime import date, datetime\n29\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n30\tfrom pathlib import Path\n31\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\n32\t\n33\timport click\n34\t\n35\tfrom packages.application.analysis.ports.cost_tracker_port import CostBudgetExceeded\n36\tfrom packages.contracts.types import Ticker\n37\tfrom packages.domain.analysis.models.perspective_analysis import PerspectiveAnalysis\n38\tfrom packages.domain.analysis.models.thesis import BearCaseInvariantError, ThesisStatus\n39\t\n40\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n41\t    pass\n42\t\n43\t# Internal type alias for perspective helper\n44\t_Perspective = PerspectiveAnalysis\n45\t\n46\t_DISCLAIMER_MD = (\n47\t    \"\\n---\\n\\n\"\n48\t    \"> **Disclaimer (I-S35)**: This is a research aid, not financial advice. \"\n49\t    \"Decisions and responsibility are yours. \"\n50\t    \"Numbers come from deterministic code; narrative from LLM interpretation. \"\n51\t    \"Past performance does not predict future results. \"\n52\t    \"Calibration grade: D (Phase 2 — no calibration data yet, BR-7).\\n\"\n53\t)\n54\t\n55\t\n56\t@click.command()\n57\t@click.option(\"--ticker\", required=True, help=\"VN30 ticker symbol (e.g. HPG, VHM)\")\n58\t@click.option(\n59\t    \"--as-of\",\n60\t    default=None,\n61\t    type=click.DateTime(formats=[\"%Y-%m-%d\"]),\n62\t    help=\"Analysis date (YYYY-MM-DD; default = today)\",\n63\t)\n64\t@click.option(\n65\t    \"--db\",\n66\t    default=\"data/stockforge.sqlite\",\n67\t    show_default=True,\n68\t    type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path),\n69\t    help=\"SQLite database path\",\n70\t)\n71\t@click.option(\n72\t    \"--output-dir\",\n73\t    default=\"agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/\",\n74\t    show_default=True,\n75\t    type=click.Path(file_okay=False, path_type=Path),\n76\t    help=\"Directory where thesis markdown is written\",\n77\t)\n78\t@click.option(\n79\t    \"--max-cost-usd\",\n80\t    default=\"3.00\",\n81\t    show_default=True,\n82\t    help=\"Hard cost cap per execution in USD (BR-6 / I-40)\",\n83\t)\n84\t@click.option(\n85\t    \"--mock-llm/--no-mock-llm\",\n86\t    default=False,\n87\t    help=\"Use mock LLM returning canned PerspectiveAnalysis (for tests/CI)\",\n88\t)\n89\t@click.option(\n90\t    \"--transport\",\n91\t    type=click.Choice([\"anthropic\", \"subagent\"], case_sensitive=False),\n92\t    default=\"anthropic\",\n93\t    show_default=True,\n94\t    help=\"LLM substrate when --no-mock-llm: 'anthropic' (SDK; needs API key) or \"\n95\t    \"'subagent' (claude CLI subprocess; uses subscription, no key required; S43b)\",\n96\t)\n97\tdef main(\n98\t    ticker: str,\n99\t    as_of: datetime | None,\n100\t    db: Path,\n101\t    output_dir: Path,\n102\t    max_cost_usd: str,\n103\t    mock_llm: bool,\n104\t    transport: str,\n105\t) -> None:\n106\t    \"\"\"Validate a thesis for TICKER and write markdown to OUTPUT_DIR.\"\"\"\n107\t    _ensure_utf8()\n108\t\n109\t    effective_as_of: date = as_of.date() if as_of is not None else date.today()\n110\t    max_cost = Decimal(max_cost_usd)\n111\t    ticker_upper = ticker.strip().upper()\n112\t\n113\t    click.echo(\n114\t        f\"[validate_thesis] ticker={ticker_upper} as_of={effective_as_of} \"\n115\t        f\"mock_llm={mock_llm} transport={transport} db={db} max_cost=${max_cost}\"\n116\t    )\n117\t\n118\t    from apps._shared.use_case_builder import BuildError, build_use_case  # noqa: PLC0415\n119\t\n120\t    try:\n121\t        use_case = build_use_case(\n122\t            db_path=db,\n123\t            mock_llm=mock_llm,\n124\t            max_cost_usd=max_cost,\n125\t            transport=transport.lower(),\n126\t        )\n127\t    except BuildError as exc:\n128\t        click.echo(f\"[validate_thesis] ERROR: {exc}\", err=True)\n129\t        sys.exit(4)\n130\t    except NotImplementedError as exc:\n131\t        click.echo(f\"[validate_thesis] ERROR (DOGFOOD gate): {exc}\", err=True)\n132\t        sys.exit(3)\n133\t\n134\t    try:\n135\t        thesis = asyncio.run(\n136\t            use_case.execute(Ticker(ticker_upper), effective_as_of)\n137\t        )\n138\t    except CostBudgetExceeded as exc:\n139\t        click.echo(\n140\t            f\"[validate_thesis] COST CAP EXCEEDED: spent ${exc.spent:.4f} \"\n141\t            f\"> limit ${exc.limit:.4f}\",\n142\t            err=True,\n143\t        )\n144\t        sys.exit(1)\n145\t    except BearCaseInvariantError as exc:\n146\t        click.echo(f\"[validate_thesis] BEAR CASE INVARIANT FAILED: {exc}\", err=True)\n147\t        sys.exit(2)\n148\t\n149\t    # Write thesis to markdown\n150\t    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n151\t    out_path = output_dir / f\"{effective_as_of}-{ticker_upper}.md\"\n152\t    md = _render_thesis_md(thesis)\n153\t    out_path.write_text(md, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n154\t\n155\t    click.echo(f\"[validate_thesis] thesis written: {out_path}\")\n156\t\n157\t    if thesis.status == ThesisStatus.INCOMPLETE:\n158\t        gaps_list = list(thesis.gaps)\n159\t        click.echo(\n160\t            f\"[validate_thesis] WARNING: thesis is INCOMPLETE — gaps: {gaps_list}\",\n161\t            err=True,\n162\t        )\n163\t        # Cost exceeded path: use case caught CostBudgetExceeded internally and\n164\t        # returned Thesis.incomplete(gaps=[\"cost_budget_exceeded\"]) (BR-6).\n165\t        if \"cost_budget_exceeded\" in thesis.gaps:\n166\t            click.echo(\n167\t                \"[validate_thesis] COST CAP EXCEEDED — thesis not persisted (BR-6 / I-40).\",\n168\t                err=True,\n169\t            )\n170\t diacritics preserved (no stripping).\n\nHARD BINDING (S43a-CODE):\n- mock_llm defaults True for development; set STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=false + ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\n  for dogfood (pending Q&A 2026-05-01-002 gate).\n\nSource: specs/tier2-feature/006-phase-2-track-F-thesis-pipeline.md § B.7\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport os\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport streamlit as st  # type: ignore[import-not-found]\n\nfrom packages.application.analysis.ports.cost_tracker_port import CostBudgetExceeded\nfrom packages.contracts.types import Ticker\n\n_DEFAULT_DB = Path(\"data/stockforge.sqlite\")\n_DEFAULT_MAX_COST = Decimal(\"3.00\")\n_DISCLAIMER_FOOTER = (\n    \"Research aid — not financial advice (I-S35). \"\n    \"Calibration grade: D (Phase 2 heuristic; no calibration data yet).\"\n)\n\n\ndef render() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Render the Validate Thesis page.\"\"\"\n    st.title(\"Validate Thesis\")\n    st.caption(\"Run a 3-perspective analysis (Bear / Bull / Quant) on a VN30 ticker.\")\n\n    # --- Inputs ---\n    ticker_raw = st.text_input(\"Ticker\", placeholder=\"e.g. HPG, VHM, FPT\")\n    as_of_val: date = st.date_input(\"As of\", value=date.today())\n\n    mock_llm = os.environ.get(\"STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM\", \"true\").lower() != \"false\"\n    db_path = Path(os.environ.get(\"STOCKFORGE_DB\", str(_DEFAULT_DB)))\n    max_cost_usd = Decimal(os.environ.get(\"STOCKFORGE_MAX_COST_USD\", str(_DEFAULT_MAX_COST)))\n\n    if st.button(\"Validate\"):\n        ticker_str = ticker_raw.strip().upper()\n        if not ticker_str:\n            st.error(\"Please enter a ticker symbol.\")\n            return\n\n        _run_validation(\n            ticker_str=ticker_str,\n            as_of=as_of_val,\n            db_path=db_path,\n            mock_llm=mock_llm,\n            max_cost_usd=max_cost_usd,\n        )\n\n\ndef _run_validation(\n    *,\n    ticker_str: str,\n    as_of: date,\n    db_path: Path,\n    mock_llm: bool,\n    max_cost_usd: Decimal,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Execute the use case and render results (or errors).\"\"\"\n    from apps._shared.use_case_builder import BuildError, build_use_case  # noqa: PLC0415\n    from apps.dashboard.components.thesis_card import render_thesis_card  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    with st.spinner(\n        \"Running thesis validation... cost cap $3 / time cap 5 min. Please wait.\"\n    ):\n        try:\n            use_case = build_use_case(\n                db_path=db_path,\n                mock_llm=mock_llm,\n                max_cost_usd=max_cost_usd,\n            )\n        except BuildError as exc:\n            st.error(f\"Configuration error: {exc}\")\n            return\n        except NotImplementedError as exc:\n            st.error(f\"Live analysis gate not yet open: {exc}\")\n            return\n\n        try:\n            thesis = asyncio.run(\n                use_case.execute(Ticker(ticker_str), as_of)\n            )\n        except CostBudgetExceeded as exc:\n            st.error(\n                f\"Cost budget exceeded: spent ${exc.spent:.4f} \"\n                f\"(limit ${exc.limit:.4f}). Partial results not saved.\"\n            )\n            return\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n            st.error(f\"Unexpected error during thesis validation: {exc}\")\n            return\n\n    render_thesis_card(thesis)\n\n    # Footer\n    cost_str = f\"${thesis.cost_usd:.4f}\"\n    conf_str = str(thesis.confidence_level) if thesis.confidence_level else \"n/a\"\n    st.caption(\n        f\"thesis_id: `{thesis.thesis_id[:16]}...` | \"\n        f\"cost: {cost_str} | \"\n        f\"confidence: {conf_str} | \"\n        f\"{_DISCLAIMER_FOOTER}\"\n    )\n","numLines":122,"startLine":1,"totalLines":122}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"39e1097a-6ccf-419b-bf81-94bcb63d5837","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

annotations\n\nfrom collections.abc import Iterator\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom click.testing import CliRunner\n\nfrom packages.application.analysis.ports.cost_tracker_port import (\n    Budget,\n    CostBudgetExceeded,\n)\nfrom packages.contracts.types import Ticker\nfrom packages.domain.analysis.models.perspective_analysis import (\n    PerspectiveAnalysis,\n    PerspectiveRole,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.analysis.models.synthesis import (\n    Confluence,\n packages.domain.analysis.value_objects.conviction import Conviction\nfrom packages.domain.analysis.value_objects.grounded_point import GroundedPoint\nfrom packages.domain.analysis.value_objects.trade_off_matrix import (\n    DimensionVerdict,\n    TradeOffMatrix,\n)\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Shared fixtures and helpers\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_TODAY = date.today()\n_TICKER_STR = \"HPG\"\n\n\ndef _pt(text: str = \"claim\", cat: str | None = None) -> GroundedPoint:\n    return GroundedPoint(\n        text=text,\n        source_url=\"https://mock.example.com/filing\",\n        source_excerpt=\"Verbatim excerpt for testing purposes only.\",\n        as_of=_TODAY,\n        conviction=Conviction.MODERATE,\n        category=cat,\n    )\n\n\ndef _pa(role: PerspectiveRole, points: tuple[GroundedPoint, ...]) -> PerspectiveAnalysis:\n    return PerspectiveAnalysis(\n        role=role,\n        key_points=points,\n        overall_conviction=Conviction.MODERATE,\n        cost_usd=Decimal(\"0.10\"),\n        model_id=\"mock-llm\",\n        prompt_hash=\"deadbeef01234567\",\n    )\n\n\ndef _good_bear() -> PerspectiveAnalysis:\n    return _pa(\n        PerspectiveRole.BEAR,\n        (\n            _pt(\"Fundamental risk\", BearCategory.FUNDAMENTAL),\n            _pt(\"Valuation risk\", BearCategory.VALUATION),\n            _pt(\"Macro risk\", BearCategory.MACRO),\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef _good_bull() -> PerspectiveAnalysis:\n    return _pa(PerspectiveRole.BULL, (_pt(\"Growth\"), _pt(\"Moat\"), _pt(\"Cashflow\")))\n\n\ndef _good_quant() -> PerspectiveAnalysis:\n    return _pa(PerspectiveRole.QUANT, (_pt(\"P/B below avg\"), _pt(\"ROE above peer\")))\n\n\ndef _synthesis() -> Synthesis:\n    matrix = TradeOffMatrix(\n        scores={d: DimensionVerdict.NEUTRAL for d in (\"VALUE\", \"QUALITY\", \"GROWTH\", \"RISK\")},\n        evidence={d: () for d in (\"VALUE\", \"QUALITY\", \"GROWTH\", \"RISK\")},\n    )\n    return Synthesis(\n        trade_off_matrix=matrix,\n        confluence_assessment=Confluence.MIXED,\n        explicit_disagreements=(),\n        catalysts=(),\n        risks=(),\n        reasoning_trace=\"mock reasoning trace\",\n    )\n\n\nclass _MockLLMAgent:\n    \"\"\"Returns a fixed PerspectiveAnalysis for any call.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, pa: PerspectiveAnalysis) -> None:\n        self._pa = pa\n\n    async def analyze(self, _ticker: object, _ctx: object, _role: object) -> PerspectiveAnalysis:\n        return self._pa\n\n\nclass _InsufficientBearAgent:\n    \"\"\"Returns <3-point bear first call, then good bear on second call.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self._calls = 0\n\n    async def analyze(self, _ticker: object, _ctx: object, _role: object) -> PerspectiveAnalysis:\n        self._calls += 1\n        if self._calls == 1:\n            # Insufficient — only 1 point with 1 category\n            return _pa(\n                PerspectiveRole.BEAR,\n                (_pt(\"only one point\", BearCategory.FUNDAMENTAL),),\n            )\n        # Still insufficient on second call → triggers BearCaseInvariantError path\n        return _pa(\n            PerspectiveRole.BEAR,\n            (_pt(\"still only one\", BearCategory.FUNDAMENTAL),),\n        )\n\n\nclass _MockSynthesizer:\n    def __init__(self, synthesis: Synthesis) -> None:\n        self._synthesis = synthesis\n\n    async def synthesize(self, _ticker: object, _persp: object, _ctx: object) -> Synthesis:\n        return self._synthesis\n\n\nclass _MockThesisRepo:\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.saved: list[Thesis] = []\n\n    async def save(self, thesis: Thesis) -> None:\n        self.saved.append(thesis)\n\n    async def get_by_id(self, thesis_id: str) -> Thesis | None:\n        return next((t for t in self.saved if t.thesis_id == thesis_id), None)\n\n    async def find_by_ticker(self, ticker: Ticker, as_of: date) -> list[Thesis]:\n        return [t for t in self.saved if t.ticker == ticker and t.as_of <= as_of]\n\n\nclass _MockEventBus:\n    async def publish(self, _event: object) -> None:\n        pass\n\n\nclass _MockCostTracker:\n    def __init__(self, limit: Decimal = Decimal(\"3.00\")) -> None:\n        self.limit = limit\n\n    @contextmanager\n    def scoped_budget(self, limit_usd: Decimal) -> Iterator[Budget]:\n        budget = Budget(spent=Decimal(\"0\"), limit=limit_usd)\n        try:\n            yield budget\n        except CostBudgetExceeded:\n            raise\n\n    async def record(self, usd: Decimal) -> None:\n        pass\n\n\nclass _MockDataGatherer:\n    def __init__(self, gaps: list[str] | None = None) -> None:\n        self._gaps = gaps or []\n\n    async def gather(self, ticker: Ticker, as_of: date) -> object:\n        from packages.application.analysis.use_cases.validate_thesis_phase1 import (\n            SharedContext,  # noqa: PLC0415\n        )\n\n        return SharedContext(\n            ticker=ticker,\n            as_of=as_of,\n            quotes=[\"bar\"],\n            statements=object(),\n            data_snapshot_md5=\"testmd5abc\",\n            gaps=self._gaps,\n        )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Patch helper: inject mocked use case into build_use_case\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _make_use_case(\n    *,\n    bear: object | None = None,\n    bull: object | None = None,\n    quant: object | None = None,\n    synthesizer: object | None = None,\n    data_gatherer: object | None = None,\n    cost_tracker: object | None = None,\n) -> object:\n    from packages.application.analysis.use_cases.validate_thesis_phase1 import (  # noqa: PLC0415\n        ValidateThesisPhase1UseCase,\n    )\n\n    return ValidateThesisPhase1UseCase(\n        data_gatherer=data_gatherer or _MockDataGatherer(),\n        bear_agent=bear or _MockLLMAgent(_good_bear()),\n        bull_agent=bull or _MockLLMAgent(_good_bull()),\n        quant_agent=quant or _MockLLMAgent(_good_quant()),\n        synthesizer=synthesizer or _MockSynthesizer(_synthesis()),\n        thesis_repo=_MockThesisRepo(),\n        cost_tracker=cost_tracker or _MockCostTracker(),\n        event_bus=_MockEventBus(),\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Tests\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_t1_mock_llm_happy_path(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    \"\"\"t1: --mock-llm happy path; exit 0; thesis file created with required frontmatter.\"\"\"\n    from apps.cli.validate_thesis import main  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    use_case = _make_use_case()\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\n        \"apps._shared.use_case_builder.build_use_case\",\n        lambda **_kwargs: use_case,\n    )\n\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--ticker\", \"HPG\",\n            \"--as-of\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--mock-llm\",\n            \"--db\", str(tmp_path / \"test.sqlite\"),\n            \"--output-dir\", str(tmp_path / The CLI detects this gap and exits 1 (BR-6).\n    \"\"\"\n    from apps.cli.validate_thesis import main  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    # Return a pre-built INCOMPLETE thesis with cost_budget_exceeded gap\n    from packages.domain.analysis.models.thesis import Thesis  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    incomplete_thesis = Thesis.incomplete(\n        Ticker(\"HPG\"),\n        date(2026, 4, 29),\n        gaps=[\"cost_budget_exceeded\"],\n    )\n\n    class _CostCapUseCase:\n        async def execute(self, _ticker: object, _as_of: object) -> Thesis:\n            return incomplete_thesis\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\n        \"apps._shared.use_case_builder.build_use_case\",\n        lambda **_kwargs: _CostCapUseCase(),\n    )\n\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--ticker\", \"HPG\",\n            \"--as-of\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--mock-llm\",\n            \"--db\", str(tmp_path / \"test.sqlite\"),\n            \"--output-dir\", str(tmp_path / \"thesis-log\"),\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 1, f\"Expected exit 1; got {result.exit_code}. noqa: PLC0415\n    from packages.domain.analysis.models.thesis import Thesis  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    incomplete_bear = Thesis.incomplete(\n        Ticker(\"HPG\"),\n        date(2026, 4, 29),\n        gaps=[\"bear_case_insufficient\"],\n    )\n\n    class _BearFailUseCase:\n        async def execute(self, _ticker: object, _as_of: object) -> Thesis:\n            return incomplete_bear\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\n        \"apps._shared.use_case_builder.build_use_case\",\n        lambda **_kwargs: _BearFailUseCase(),\n    )\n\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--ticker\", \"HPG\",\n            \"--as-of\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--mock-llm\",\n            \"--db\", str(tmp_path / \"test.sqlite\"),\n            \"--output-dir\", str(tmp_path / \"thesis-log\"),\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 2, f\"Expected exit 2; got {result.exit_code}. {"parentUuid":"ce06da20-7c8b-4011-88a0-d2ab38fcd774","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01QcnTE3gGZGg3nMku5HE71t","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01FTikgi6YBVSNbgoeK4wKZk","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\apps\\cli\\test_validate_thesis.py","old_string":"def test_t7_no_mock_llm_without_api_key_exits_4(\n    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"t7: --no-mock-llm without ANTHROPIC_API_KEY → exits 4 with helpful error.\"\"\"\n    from apps.cli.validate_thesis import main  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    # Ensure key is absent\n    monkeypatch.delenv(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\", raising=False)\n\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--ticker\", \"HPG\",\n            \"--as-of\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--no-mock-llm\",\n            \"--db\", str(tmp_path / \"test.sqlite\"),\n            \"--output-dir\", str(tmp_path / \"thesis-log\"),\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 4, (\n        f\"Expected exit 4 (BuildError for missing API key); \"\n        f\"got {result.exit_code}. Output:\\n{result.output}\"\n    )\n    # Helpful error message must mention ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\n    combined_output = result.output or \"\"\n    assert \"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\" in combined_output, (\n        f\"Expected 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY' in error output; got:\\n{combined_output}\"\n    )","new_string":"def test_t7_anthropic_transport_explicit_deprecated_exits_3(\n    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"t7 (S227 reframe): --no-mock-llm --transport=anthropic → exits 3 with\n    deprecation pointer to canonical subagent path.\n\n    Per S227 user directive (2026-05-09): every ANTHROPIC_API_KEY usage is\n    replaced by Claude Code subagent dispatch. Default --no-mock-llm now\n    routes via subagent (no key required); explicit --transport=anthropic\n    raises NotImplementedError pointing the caller back to subagent.\n    \"\"\"\n    from apps.cli.validate_thesis import main  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    # Whether or not the key is present is irrelevant under the new doctrine\n    monkeypatch.delenv(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\", raising=False)\n\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--ticker\", \"HPG\",\n            \"--as-of\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--no-mock-llm\",\n            \"--transport\", \"anthropic\",\n            \"--db\", str(tmp_path / \"test.sqlite\"),\n            \"--output-dir\", str(tmp_path / \"thesis-log\"),\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 3, (\n        f\"Expected exit 3 (NotImplementedError for deprecated anthropic transport); \"\n        f\"got {result.exit_code}. Output:\\n{result.output}\"\n    )\n    combined_output = result.output or \"\"\n    assert \"subagent\" in combined_output.lower(), (\n        f\"Expected 'subagent' pointer in deprecation error; got:\\n{combined_output}\"\n    )"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":958,"cache_read_input_tokens":165151,"output_tokens":1914,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":958,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1914,"cache_read_input_tokens":165151,"cache_creation_input_tokens":958,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":958},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CasCebbtNhTt6n5B5Kfqa","type":"assistant","uuid":"5a0eca8c-05b6-4f5b-8114-5622634b4c05","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:04:58.070Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"b305b705-31cc-4950-b2ee-3e0e48cdb7a7","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"079aebaa-bf67-4e85-8846-668508ad386d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Jhnf9WKg7zdiaqLaD1NubW","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\apps\\dashboard\\pages\\validate_thesis.py has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"faa345e5-a390-4ab1-8385-5e1855c536ab","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:05:03.336Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\apps\\dashboard\\pages\\validate_thesis.py","oldString":"HARD BINDING (S43a-CODE):\n- mock_llm defaults True for development; set STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=false + ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\n  for dogfood (pending Q&A 2026-05-01-002 gate).","newString":"HARD BINDING (S227 — anthropic→subagent default flip; supersedes S43a):\n- mock_llm defaults True for development; set STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=false to\n  route LIVE LLM calls. NO ANTHROPIC_API_KEY required — default transport is\n  the claude CLI subprocess substrate (Claude Code subscription) per user\n  directive 2026-05-09 (\"for every ANTHROPIC_API_KEY use subagent instead\").","originalFile":"\"\"\"Validate Thesis page — Streamlit BC-8 Analysis UI.\n\nComposition root: builds ValidateThesisPhase1UseCase via shared use_case_builder.\nRuns asyncio.run(use_case.execute(...)) inside button handler.\nRenders thesis card via apps.dashboard.components.thesis_card.render_thesis_card.\n\nCost-aware:\n- Catches CostBudgetExceeded → st.error with partial cost.\n- Footer always shows thesis_id + cost_usd + confidence_level + I-S35 reminder.\n\nVietnamese-aware: UTF-8 throughout; diacritics preserved (no stripping).\n\nHARD BINDING (S43a-CODE):\n- mock_llm defaults True for development; set STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=false + ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\n  for dogfood (pending Q&A 2026-05-01-002 gate).\n\nSource: specs/tier2-feature/006-phase-2-track-F-thesis-pipeline.md § B.7\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport os\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport streamlit as st  # type: ignore[import-not-found]\n\nfrom packages.application.analysis.ports.cost_tracker_port import CostBudgetExceeded\nfrom packages.contracts.types import Ticker\n\n_DEFAULT_DB = Path(\"data/stockforge.sqlite\")\n_DEFAULT_MAX_COST = Decimal(\"3.00\")\n_DISCLAIMER_FOOTER = (\n    \"Research aid — not financial advice (I-S35). \"\n    \"Calibration grade: D (Phase 2 heuristic; no calibration data yet).\"\n)\n\n\ndef render() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Render the Validate Thesis page.\"\"\"\n    st.title(\"Validate Thesis\")\n    st.caption(\"Run a 3-perspective analysis (Bear / Bull / Quant) on a VN30 ticker.\")\n\n    # --- Inputs ---\n    ticker_raw = st.text_input(\"Ticker\", placeholder=\"e.g. HPG, VHM, FPT\")\n    as_of_val: date = st.date_input(\"As of\", value=date.today())\n\n    mock_llm = os.environ.get(\"STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM\", \"true\").lower() != \"false\"\n    db_path = Path(os.environ.get(\"STOCKFORGE_DB\", str(_DEFAULT_DB)))\n    max_cost_usd = Decimal(os.environ.get(\"STOCKFORGE_MAX_COST_USD\", str(_DEFAULT_MAX_COST)))\n\n    if st.button(\"Validate\"):\n        ticker_str = ticker_raw.strip().upper()\n        if not ticker_str:\n            st.error(\"Please enter a ticker symbol.\")\n            return\n\n        _run_validation(\n            ticker_str=ticker_str,\n            as_of=as_of_val,\n            db_path=db_path,\n            mock_llm=mock_llm,\n            max_cost_usd=max_cost_usd,\n        )\n\n\ndef _run_validation(\n    *,\n    ticker_str: str,\n    as_of: date,\n    db_path: Path,\n    mock_llm: bool,\n    max_cost_usd: Decimal,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Execute the use case and render results (or errors).\"\"\"\n    from apps._shared.use_case_builder import BuildError, build_use_case  # noqa: PLC0415\n    from apps.dashboard.components.thesis_card import render_thesis_card  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    with st.spinner(\n        \"Running thesis validation... cost cap $3 / time cap 5 min. Please wait.\"\n    ):\n        try:\n            use_case = build_use_case(\n                db_path=db_path,\n                mock_llm=mock_llm,\n                max_cost_usd=max_cost_usd,\n            )\n        except BuildError as exc:\n            st.error(f\"Configuration error: {exc}\")\n            return\n        except NotImplementedError as exc:\n            st.error(f\"Live analysis gate not yet open: {exc}\")\n            return\n\n        try:\n            thesis = asyncio.run(\n                use_case.execute(Ticker(ticker_str), as_of)\n            )\n        except CostBudgetExceeded as exc:\n            st.error(\n                f\"Cost budget exceeded: spent ${exc.spent:.4f} \"\n                f\"(limit ${exc.limit:.4f}). Partial results not saved.\"\n            )\n            return\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n            st.error(f\"Unexpected error during thesis validation: {exc}\")\n            return\n\n    render_thesis_card(thesis)\n\n    # Footer\n    cost_str = f\"${thesis.cost_usd:.4f}\"\n    conf_str = str(thesis.confidence_level) if thesis.confidence_level else \"n/a\"\n    st.caption(\n        f\"thesis_id: `{thesis.thesis_id[:16]}...` | \"\n        f\"cost: {cost_str} | \"\n        f\"confidence: {conf_str} | \"\n        f\"{_DISCLAIMER_FOOTER}\"\n    )\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":10,"oldLines":9,"newStart":10,"newLines":11,"lines":[" "," Vietnamese-aware: UTF-8 throughout; diacritics preserved (no stripping)."," ","-HARD BINDING (S43a-CODE):","-- mock_llm defaults True for development; set STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=false + ANTHROPIC_API_KEY","-  for dogfood (pending Q&A 2026-05-01-002 gate).","+HARD BINDING (S227 — anthropic→subagent default flip; supersedes S43a):","+- mock_llm defaults True for development; set STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=false to","+  route LIVE LLM calls. NO ANTHROPIC_API_KEY required — default transport is","+  the claude CLI subprocess substrate (Claude Code subscription) per user","+  directive 2026-05-09 (\"for every ANTHROPIC_API_KEY use subagent instead\")."," "," Source: specs/tier2-feature/006-phase-2-track-F-thesis-pipeline.md § B.7"," \"\"\""]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b305b705-31cc-4950-b2ee-3e0e48cdb7a7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

flip news/claude_llm_extractor default same way\"\n    blocking: false\n  - id: D-052-anthropic-sdk-removal\n    summary: \"After D-051 lands, remove _default_transport stubs + drop anthropic from pyproject.toml dependencies\"\n    blocking: false\n\n---\n\n# D-050 — Replace ANTHROPIC_API_KEY with Claude Code subagent dispatch (systemic)\n\n## Summary\n\nPer user directive 2026-05-09 (\"no need anthropic api key, use claude code subagent (claude subscription) instead, for every 'anthropic api key'\"): the canonical LLM substrate for stockforge production code is the **claude CLI subprocess** (parent Claude Code OAuth/keychain), NOT the `anthropic` SDK with `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. This rule is **systemic** — applies to every site that would otherwise reach for the SDK + key.\n\n## Implementation (this session — S227)\n\nSurgical default-flip leveraging existing S43b substrate:\n\n1. `apps/_shared/use_case_builder.py`:\n   - Default `transport: str = \"anthropic\"` → `\"subagent\"`.\n   - `_DOGFOOD_GATE_MSG` rewritten as deprecation pointer.\n   - `_check_live_gate()` simplified to always raise `NotImplementedError` when `transport=='anthropic'` (env-var check removed; no longer relevant).\n   - HARD BINDING docstring rewritten.\n\n2. `apps/cli/validate_thesis.py`:\n   - `--transport` default `\"anthropic\"` → `\"subagent\"`.\n   - HARD BINDING + exit-code docstring rewritten.\n\n3. `apps/cli/test_validate_thesis.py`:\n   - `t7` rewritten: was `--no-mock-llm without ANTHROPIC_API_KEY → exit 4 + key in error`; now `--no-mock-llm --transport=anthropic → exit 3 + 'subagent' pointer in error`.\n\n4. `apps/dashboard/pages/validate_thesis.py`:\n   - HARD BINDING docstring rewritten.\n\n## Empirical close-verify\n\n- `python -m py_compile` all 4 files = OK.\n- `pytest apps/cli/test_validate_thesis.py -q` = **7/7 PASS** in 0.14s.\n- `pytest packages/infrastructure/analysis/ packages/application/analysis/ packages/domain/analysis/ apps/_shared/ apps/cli/test_validate_thesis.py apps/dashboard/test_smoke.py -q` = **94 PASS / 8 skipped** (skipped = streamlit dashboard smoke; not installed in dev env).\n- `bash scripts/hooks/firing-tests/run-all.sh` = **86/86 PASS** in 235s zero regression vs S226 baseline.\n\n## Deferred (separate ADRs)\n\n- **D-051**: News claim extractor refactor — `packages/infrastructure/news/claude_llm_extractor.py` uses a different transport signature `(prompt, body) → str`. Need a sibling `claude_cli_news_transport` module before flipping its default. Production ingest path; not load-bearing for current Phase 3 close.\n- **D-052**: Anthropic SDK code-path removal — after D-051 lands, delete `_default_transport` stubs from both adapter modules and drop `anthropic` from `pyproject.toml` dependencies.\n\n## LIVE-dogfood gate impact\n\nThe Phase 3 LIVE 5-KOL+5-ticker dogfood gate (Q&A 2026-05-01-002 + master-plan §S57) was previously gated on `$8-15 ANTHROPIC_API_KEY budget authorization`. Under the new doctrine the marginal cost is **~$0** (subscription already paid), so the gate semantics shift to \"**streamlit installed + sqlite populated via ingest CLIs**\" — re-fire authorization next session once those prereqs land.\n\n## Charter binding\n\nThis ADR is CHARTER-class because it codifies a systemic LLM-routing rule across the entire codebase. Promotion-target candidate for `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` § LLM Substrate (or new file `constitution/llm-routing.md`) at next promote-rule cycle.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1996,"cache_read_input_tokens":169949,"output_tokens":3753,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1996,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3753,"cache_read_input_tokens":169949,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1996,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1996},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CasD1hXfLwkvoUFNpT3Vb","type":"assistant","uuid":"b91e12dd-b6a4-4c33-a747-6236dd77d478","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:10:28.231Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

This rule is **systemic** — applies to every site that would otherwise reach for the SDK + key.\n\n## Implementation (this session — S227)\n\nSurgical default-flip leveraging existing S43b substrate:\n\n1. `apps/_shared/use_case_builder.py`:\n   - Default `transport: str = \"anthropic\"` → `\"subagent\"`.\n   - `_DOGFOOD_GATE_MSG` rewritten as deprecation pointer.\n   - `_check_live_gate()` simplified to always raise `NotImplementedError` when `transport=='anthropic'` (env-var check removed; no longer relevant).\n   - HARD BINDING docstring rewritten.\n\n2. `apps/cli/validate_thesis.py`:\n   - `--transport` default `\"anthropic\"` → `\"subagent\"`.\n   - HARD BINDING + exit-code docstring rewritten.\n\n3. `apps/cli/test_validate_thesis.py`:\n   - `t7` rewritten: was `--no-mock-llm without ANTHROPIC_API_KEY → exit 4 + key in error`; now `--no-mock-llm --transport=anthropic → exit 3 + 'subagent' pointer in error`.\n\n4. `apps/dashboard/pages/validate_thesis.py`:\n   - HARD BINDING docstring rewritten.\n\n## Empirical close-verify\n\n- `python -m py_compile` all 4 files = OK.\n- `pytest apps/cli/test_validate_thesis.py -q` = **7/7 PASS** in 0.14s.\n- `pytest packages/infrastructure/analysis/ packages/application/analysis/ packages/domain/analysis/ apps/_shared/ apps/cli/test_validate_thesis.py apps/dashboard/test_smoke.py -q` = **94 PASS / 8 skipped** (skipped = streamlit dashboard smoke; not installed in dev env).\n- `bash scripts/hooks/firing-tests/run-all.sh` = **86/86 PASS** in 235s zero regression vs S226 baseline.\n\n## Deferred (separate ADRs)\n\n- **D-051**: News claim extractor refactor — `packages/infrastructure/news/claude_llm_extractor.py` uses a different transport signature `(prompt, body) → str`. Need a sibling `claude_cli_news_transport` module before flipping its default. Production ingest path; not load-bearing for current Phase 3 close.\n- **D-052**: Anthropic SDK code-path removal — after D-051 lands, delete `_default_transport` stubs from both adapter modules and drop `anthropic` from `pyproject.toml` dependencies.\n\n## LIVE-dogfood gate impact\n\nThe Phase 3 LIVE 5-KOL+5-ticker dogfood gate (Q&A 2026-05-01-002 + master-plan §S57) was previously gated on `$8-15 ANTHROPIC_API_KEY budget authorization`. Under the new doctrine the marginal cost is **~$0** (subscription already paid), so the gate semantics shift to \"**streamlit installed + sqlite populated via ingest CLIs**\" — re-fire authorization next session once those prereqs land.\n\n## Charter binding\n\nThis ADR is CHARTER-class because it codifies a systemic LLM-routing rule across the entire codebase. Promotion-target candidate for `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` § LLM Substrate (or new file `constitution/llm-routing.md`) at next promote-rule cycle.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b91e12dd-b6a4-4c33-a747-6236dd77d478","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

Verbatim user directive 2026-05-09. Phrase \"for every\" = systemic rule, not one-off override. Substrate already shipped at S43b (subagent_transport.py + _build_subagent_agents in use_case_builder + r-2026-05-01-claude-cli-substrate.md) but was OPT-IN via `--transport=subagent` flag; default was still `anthropic`. New doctrine = subagent is canonical default; anthropic transport string raises NotImplementedError pointing back to subagent.\n**Rule**: NEVER add `import anthropic`, `anthropic.Anthropic()`, or `os.environ[\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\"]` in production code (apps/, packages/) without surrounding deprecation guard. Default LLM substrate = `claude` CLI subprocess via `packages/infrastructure/analysis/subagent_transport.py::claude_cli_transport` (or sibling for different transport signatures). Subscription billed; ~$0 marginal cost vs API metered $$. Mock LLM (`STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=true`) remains valid for tests.\n**Anti-example**: `_default_transport = lambda ...: anthropic.Anthropic().messages.create(...)` as the DEFAULT path — silently consumes ANTHROPIC_API_KEY when present, fails confusingly when absent, and bills per-token even though user has paid Claude Code subscription. Or: gating `--no-mock-llm` on env var presence (\"set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") instead of routing to subagent.\n**Correct example**: `transport: str = \"subagent\"` as the DEFAULT in `apps/_shared/use_case_builder.py`; `_check_live_gate()` always raises `NotImplementedError(_DOGFOOD_GATE_MSG)` when explicit `transport=='anthropic'` requested, with deprecation pointer to subagent path. CLI `--transport` default = `\"subagent\"` + `Choice([\"anthropic\", \"subagent\"])` retains anthropic only as explicit-opt-in marker for future re-enable. Empirical close-verify 2026-05-09: 7/7 CLI tests + 94/94 BC-8+dashboard pytest + 86/86 firing-test PASS zero regression.\n**Severity**: critical (CHARTER-class user directive; cost model + auth model fundamentally changed; affects every future LLM-call site).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — drift-signals candidate D10: grep for `import anthropic` / `anthropic.Anthropic\\b` / `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in `apps/**/*.py` + `packages/**/*.py` (excluding `_default_transport` deprecation stubs); WARN if any production-code site still references SDK or env-var without `# noqa: D10-deprecated` marker. Pairs with promote-rule cycle that should land D-051 (news extractor refactor) + D-052 (SDK code-path removal). Charter-tier promotion target: `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` § LLM Substrate or new file `constitution/llm-routing.md`.\n\n### 2026-05-09 (S223): Sub-plan IMPL completed during phase-management cycles MUST be auto-mv from pending/ to completed/ same-turn; routing-staleness when self-attestation lags actual disk state — L-S223-1"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2063,"cache_read_input_tokens":175765,"output_tokens":1571,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2063,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1571,"cache_read_input_tokens":175765,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2063,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2063},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CasD6cyTRs5uWGgs7gEid","type":"assistant","uuid":"62ae8155-405c-4299-9ed4-687e79398fc3","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:11:05.769Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"aa03e8b6-5ee2-400d-92b7-32f435377d6e","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

New doctrine = subagent is canonical default; anthropic transport string raises NotImplementedError pointing back to subagent.\n**Rule**: NEVER add `import anthropic`, `anthropic.Anthropic()`, or `os.environ[\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\"]` in production code (apps/, packages/) without surrounding deprecation guard. Default LLM substrate = `claude` CLI subprocess via `packages/infrastructure/analysis/subagent_transport.py::claude_cli_transport` (or sibling for different transport signatures). Subscription billed; ~$0 marginal cost vs API metered $$. Mock LLM (`STOCKFORGE_MOCK_LLM=true`) remains valid for tests.\n**Anti-example**: `_default_transport = lambda ...: anthropic.Anthropic().messages.create(...)` as the DEFAULT path — silently consumes ANTHROPIC_API_KEY when present, fails confusingly when absent, and bills per-token even though user has paid Claude Code subscription. Or: gating `--no-mock-llm` on env var presence (\"set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") instead of routing to subagent.\n**Correct example**: `transport: str = \"subagent\"` as the DEFAULT in `apps/_shared/use_case_builder.py`; `_check_live_gate()` always raises `NotImplementedError(_DOGFOOD_GATE_MSG)` when explicit `transport=='anthropic'` requested, with deprecation pointer to subagent path. CLI `--transport` default = `\"subagent\"` + `Choice([\"anthropic\", \"subagent\"])` retains anthropic only as explicit-opt-in marker for future re-enable. Empirical close-verify 2026-05-09: 7/7 CLI tests + 94/94 BC-8+dashboard pytest + 86/86 firing-test PASS zero regression.\n**Severity**: critical (CHARTER-class user directive; cost model + auth model fundamentally changed; affects every future LLM-call site).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — drift-signals candidate D10: grep for `import anthropic` / `anthropic.Anthropic\\b` / `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in `apps/**/*.py` + `packages/**/*.py` (excluding `_default_transport` deprecation stubs); WARN if any production-code site still references SDK or env-var without `# noqa: D10-deprecated` marker. {"parentUuid":"6bff2337-31ce-4614-9651-f15796169279","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"079aebaa-bf67-4e85-8846-668508ad386d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019Tai2R4EUJ2kWmYTaGqUw9","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\CLAUDE.md has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"c70fb2ce-279d-4584-bed4-31bdb949770a","timestamp":"2026-05-09T15:11:41.637Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\CLAUDE.md","oldString":"- **Frame as research aid, not financial advice.** Use \"thesis exploration\", \"consideration\" — not \"recommendation\", \"buy\", \"sell\".","newString":"- **Frame as research aid, not financial advice.** Use \"thesis exploration\", \"consideration\" — not \"recommendation\", \"buy\", \"sell\".\n- **NO `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / no direct `anthropic` SDK calls in production code** (D-050 / L-S227-1 / sync-042). The string `transport='anthropic'` is retained only as an explicit deprecation marker — calling it raises `NotImplementedError` pointing back to subagent.","originalFile":"# StockForge — Claude Code Instructions\n\n> Always loaded. Keep concise (target <2500 tokens).\n\n## Identity\n\nYou are **Claude Code**, the primary engineering partner for the StockForge project — an AI-first investment advisory system for the Vietnamese stock market.\n\n**Primary user**: Project owner (self-use + small trusted circle).\n**Stack**: Python primary + Postgres 16 (TimescaleDB + pgvector) + Redis + Claude API. Streamlit for dashboard. NestJS only when public API needed.\n\n---\n\n## Core Principles (Karpathy 4)\n\n**P1. Think Before Coding.** State assumptions. Surface tradeoffs. Push back when simpler approach exists. Stop when confused.\n\n**P2. Simplicity First.** Minimum code that solves the problem. No speculative features. If 200 lines could be 50, rewrite.\n\n**P3. Surgical Changes.** Touch only what the task requires. Match existing style. Every changed line traces to the task.\n\n**P4. Goal-Driven Execution.** Transform imperative → verifiable goals with concrete success criteria.\n\nFull detail: `agent-workspace/constitution/karpathy-principles.md`.\n\n---\n\n## StockForge-Specific Hard Rules (read CHARTER for context)\n\n- **NO LLM math.** LLM never outputs numbers it computed. Every number comes from code with verified inputs. LLM only interprets.\n- **Every claim has source + as-of date.** No exceptions. Hallucinated numbers in finance = real money lost.\n- **Position sizing & risk management are deterministic code.** LLM cannot override max position size, sector concentration, stop loss rules.\n- **Output is structured, not narrative.** Use trade-off matrices, multi-criteria, never single \"buy/sell\" score.\n- **Adversarial by default.** Any thesis output includes bear case explicitly. Single-perspective output is anti-pattern.\n- **Calibration over confidence.** Confidence claims must trace to historical hit rate, not to model \"feeling certain\".\n- **No insider information.** Public sources only. No paid leaks, no insider channels.\n- **Frame as research aid, not financial advice.** Use \"thesis exploration\", \"consideration\" — not \"recommendation\", \"buy\", \"sell\".\n\n---\n\n## Session Protocol\n\n### Start\n1. Read `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` → resolve active track\n2. Read `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` → project state\n3. Read last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` → recent context\n4. Check `agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/` for matching brief\n5. Run VBW protocol before writing any spec/test/code\n\n### End\n1. Update `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` (if architectural decisions made)\n2. Write `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`\n3. Update `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` (status, next session)\n4. If learned rule emerged → append to `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`\n5. If thesis logged this session → ensure entry in `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/`\n6. Update `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` with new M-S<N>-<M> entries OR explicitly state \"no mistakes this session\" in the session log (enforced by `session-end-checklist-linter.sh`)\n7. If a NEW ADR landed this session → verify `project.md` Phase Goals Tracker still matches `current-execution.md` Active Focus Track Phase status (enforced by `phase-status-coherence.sh` UserPromptSubmit cadence + `project-md-adr-staleness.sh` Stop cadence; D-038 retired Check A from latter)\n8. (auto) Stop-hook `profile-template-auto-populate.sh` appends a sample row to the matching `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/profiles/<model>-<effort>-<task_class>.md` card\n9. (auto) Stop-hook `promotion-cycle-trigger.sh` HARD-BLOCKs at next SessionStart if ≥8 new lessons accumulated since last `promote-rule` dispatch — schedule a promote-rule subagent dispatch in the next session if blocked\n\n---\n\n## Constitution (always applicable)\n\n| File | Enforces |\n|---|---|\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` | Layer boundaries, BC rules (9 BCs for stock domain) |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/invariants.md` | Stock-specific invariants (numbers, citations, no LLM math) |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/vbw-protocol.md` | Verify-Before-Write checkpoints |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md` | DR1-DR12 drift detection |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/session-budgets.md` | Token budget rules |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/boundaries.md` | What you cannot do without human approval |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/financial-data-protocol.md` | Stock-specific data integrity rules |\n\nRead these when relevant to current task.\n\n---\n\n## Hard Rules (general)\n\n- **Domain layer has ZERO framework dependency.** Pure Python in `packages/domain/`. No FastAPI, no Pydantic in domain (use dataclasses).\n- **Cross-BC communication via contracts only.** Never direct import between bounded contexts.\n- **VBW Protocol mandatory before writing specs/tests/code.** Read actual source, not memory.\n- **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. All writes go to `wiki/`.\n- **Never modify `PROJECT_CHARTER.md`.** Requires explicit human revision with version bump.\n- **Never modify files in `agent-workspace/constitution/` without explicit human approval.**\n- **Agents MUST NOT `git commit` unless user explicitly requests.** Stage changes, report, let user decide.\n- **User prompt overrides ALL defaults.** If user says \"skip X\", that trumps any skill/workflow.\n- **Context-threshold band (per D-004 — Opus 4.7 recalibrated)**: 180K wind-down (auto-prep handoff) / 220K cliff (auto-reboot via session-self-reboot.sh) / 250K hard_cap (mandatory split). Operational defaults in `scripts/hooks/budget-watchdog.sh`. Re-evaluate empirically after 10 sessions or on Anthropic model/policy change.\n- **Deterministic gates (mypy, pytest, ruff) must pass before commit.** Max 3 retry before escalate.\n- **Tracking retention (per S99 RCA Layer 1; Q-RCA-1 = A)**: `current-execution.md` ≤ 5 sessions inline / ≤ 200 LOC; `agent-notes.md` digest only / ≤ 700 LOC; `mistake-log.md` digest only / ≤ 200 LOC; `component-telemetry.jsonl` ≤ 10 MB (weekly rotate via `telemetry-rotate.sh`, retain 4 weeks). Cap breaches: WARN-only via `tracking-retention.sh` Stop hook; manual archive to dated file. Working-memory budget per `agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md` § Tier 1 = ≤ 20 KB combined for routine load.\n- **Ritual demotion (per S99 RCA Layer 5; Q-RCA-5 + Q-RCA-7 = A)**: per-session rituals (audit / re-scan / refresh) MUST track catch-rate. Catch-rate = 0 over 3+ consecutive sessions ⇒ promote-to-hook OR demote-to-passive OR retire. Refinement-of-rule (lesson-about-lesson) is AP-23 RED FLAG: 2nd instance mandates promote-or-retire (not inline accumulation).\n\n---\n\n## Dispatch Rules\n\nDo NOT hardcode phase/task paths from memory. Route through:\n- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` is the single source of truth\n- Short prompts (\"session N\", \"phase X\") resolve there\n\n---\n\n## Session Types (choose at start)\n\n| Type | Budget | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|\n| PLAN | 50-80K | Architect subagent, produces session plan |\n| FOCUSED IMPL | 100-150K | Dev, 1-3 tasks from plan |\n| MULTI-TASK IMPL | 150-250K | Dev, 4-10 tasks from plan |\n| VERIFY | 30-60K | Verifier subagent, adversarial review |\n| RECOVERY | 80-150K | Revert + re-plan after failure |\n| THESIS | 60-100K | Multi-perspective adversarial analysis on a stock |\n| INGEST | 40-80K | Process new data sources into KB |\n| POST-MORTEM | 30-50K | Review thesis outcomes, update calibration |\n\n**Never mix PLAN and IMPL in same session.** (Session 4 catastrophic failure mode.)\n**THESIS sessions are read-only on code.** Output goes to `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/`.\n\n---\n\n## Quality Gates\n\n**Tier 1 — Deterministic** (per commit, auto-block on fail):\nmypy --strict, pytest, ruff, drift-signals HIGH, dependency cycle check.\n\n**Tier 2 — Probabilistic** (per merge, separate agent):\nSpec alignment, architecture boundaries, UL consistency, code review, **calibration drift check**.\n\n**Tier 3 — Human** (per phase boundary or strategic decision):\nArchitectural decisions, API contracts, eval regression sign-off, **thesis quality review**.\n\n---\n\n## Common Anti-Patterns (avoid)\n\n- **LLM generating numbers.** `\"Based on my analysis, ROE is approximately 18%\"` — WRONG. Code must compute from data.\n- **Single-perspective thesis.** Always include bear case, even when bullish.\n- **Confident output without calibration data.** \"High confidence\" requires historical hit rate, not just model belief.\n- **Recommending stocks user already owns.** Check portfolio first; surface conflicts (confirmation bias risk).\n- **Over-fitting to recent backtest.** VN market regimes change; performance in 2021-2022 doesn't predict 2024.\n- Load entire codebase before starting (Session 4 failure) — use just-in-time loading\n- Mix plan + implement in same session (catastrophic)\n- Same-agent self-review (echo chamber) — use separate agent for Tier 2 gate\n- Write code from convention/memory — use VBW protocol\n- Refactor adjacent unrelated code (violates P3)\n- Speculative features \"for future flexibility\" (violates P2)\n\n---\n\n## Key References\n\n- **Project state**: `agent-workspace/memory/project.md`\n- **Execution router**: `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md`\n- **Learned rules**: `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`\n- **Thesis log**: `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/`\n- **Calibration data**: `agent-workspace/calibration/`\n- **Charter (immutable)**: `PROJECT_CHARTER.md`\n- **Operating manual**: `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md`\n- **Spec template**: `SPEC_TEMPLATE.md`\n- **Ubiquitous language**: `agent-workspace/ubiquitous-language/glossary.md`\n- **Skills**: `.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md`\n- **Commands**: `.claude/commands/*.md`\n- **Subagents**: `.claude/agents/*.md`\n\n---\n\n## First Interaction\n\nIf user is starting fresh, run `/session-start` to load state and suggest next actions.\nIf this is the very first session ever → guide them through `docs/DAY_1_CHECKLIST.md`.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":35,"oldLines":6,"newStart":35,"newLines":7,"lines":[" - **Calibration over confidence.** Confidence claims must trace to historical hit rate, not to model \"feeling certain\"."," - **No insider information.** Public sources only. No paid leaks, no insider channels."," - **Frame as research aid, not financial advice.** Use \"thesis exploration\", \"consideration\" — not \"recommendation\", \"buy\", \"sell\".","+- **NO `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / no direct `anthropic` SDK calls in production code** (D-050 / L-S227-1 / sync-042). Default LLM substrate = `claude` CLI subprocess via `packages/infrastructure/analysis/subagent_transport.py::claude_cli_transport` (Claude Code OAuth subscription; no API key required).
